# SOTI Snap article — Table Widget Updates in SOTI SNAP Version 2024.1
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/ka2OF0000001nPIYAY
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2024-04-08

Publish Date: 08-Apr-2024

Last Modified Date: 27-Aug-2025

SOTI SNAP

#### Summary

This article summarizes the enhancements coming to the SOTI SNAP table widget in version 2024.1.

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI SNAP

#### Environment

SNAP 2024.1.0

#### Description

The SOTI SNAP table widget is getting some significant enhancements. You will soon be able to set column widths and define text behavior including word wrap, shrink/stretch the columns to fit, or truncate the text if your user doesn’t need to see the entire content.

This greatly enhances the table viewing experience on the device. You can specify a total table width stretching beyond 100% of the device screen! A horizontal scroll bar will appear at the bottom of the table to scroll left and right to ensure the user can see all the data.

I hope you’re as excited as we are about this change. However, there are a couple of things to note once 2024.1 is released.

First, if you are using the table widget already in your app and you don’t republish the app, nothing will change on the device side. However, if you do update your app, then the table size may not be what you’d expect, so you’ll have to adjust your column sizes manually and then republish the application.

Second, you can change the behavior of the text display per table widget. This means one table may look better with word wrap turned on, while the second table will want to truncate the text if it doesn’t fit. It’s entirely up to you!

To make these changes easier, remember to preview the app on the Snap IDE. This will give you a great idea of how it’ll look on your users’ device(s) before you deploy it to everyone.

See video for more details:

# SOTI Snap article — Upcoming release of SOTI Snap (Version 2024.0.0)
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001nC76F0
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2023-09-26

Publish Date: 26-Sep-2023

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

We are happy to inform you that SOTI Snap 2024.0.0 is set to be released.

Here is a quick list of key features available in the new version:

Approval Management from Web

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

We are happy to inform you that SOTI Snap 2024.0.0 is set to be released.

Here is a quick list of key features available in the new version:

Approval Management from Web

Approval Management from Web enables users to engage with workflow records, even without a physical mobile device. By logging into the web-based IDE, users can access and review workflow specifics, track progress and take action from any Internet-connected device with browsing capabilities. With this feature, you have more convenient and flexible access to your workflow process, regardless of location or device.

User List Widget

The User List widget provides the ability to dynamically select an approver during workflow submission from a list of allowed approvers configured by an administrator. Hardcoded approvers in a workflow are no longer necessary, and you can adapt your approval process to the availability of your approvers. Consequently, app developers will not need multiple applications for different approvers, reducing extra work in the approval process.

SOTI XSight Topics

SOTI Snap has been integrated with SOTI XSight to provide SOTI XSight users real-time data from SOTI Snap on their Live View Map. This feature allows users to easily map their input widget values to a SOTI XSight topic and seamlessly pass it to SOTI XSight . SOTI XSight topics streamline your data retrieval, making it faster and more accurate with data directly from the source.

Time Zone Preferences

SOTI Snap provides users the ability to choose preferred time zones within SOTI Snap's web console preferences. If the time zone hasn't been set, SOTI Snap will check the local device's time zone and set it as the default automatically. This new feature helps mitigate potential misunderstandings and errors that could be caused by time discrepancies between app developers' local workstations and the SOTI Snap web console. 

Generate Form From PDF or Image

SOTI Snap can now automatically generate a form from a PDF or image. Users can easily create forms in a matter of minutes. Simply upload a PDF or an image (JPEG/JPG/PNG) containing the form you want to digitize and the necessary input fields and options will be populated automatically. With this new feature, you can eliminate the time spent creating a form manually and instead get started with an autogenerated form designed by SOTI Snap.

Navigator Window

The new Navigator window lets you move between widgets and pages quickly. Its simple interface shows which widgets have customizations for rules, events, actions and scripts and lets you edit them just by clicking the name, saving you time. The Navigator window even lets you search for a widget by its type or name.

The window can move the IDE and stretch, so it's never in the way when you don't need it, but it can expand to provide more information when you do. We even included a Show/Hide toggle to ensure it is only there when you need it. It truly brings the app editing experience to a whole new level.

Grid View

With our new Grid view, you can create a navigation bar at the top of the page that will load a different section of a form below or place a picture and use hotspots that will present different questions based on which hotspot you clicked. For instance, you can make a forklift inspection app that displays an image of a forklift and shows different checklists or questions based on the hotspot clicked, but the forklift picture is always present.

With our new Grid view, don’t settle for boring forms that look exactly like an outdated pen-and-paper process. Let your creative mind roam free and design native-feeling apps with SOTI Snap’s new Grid view. Transform the way you think of form-based apps by sectioning off a page to allow its windows to load a different page based on context.

Business Card Reader

Ever wish your lead retrieval app could scan potential customers’ business cards and save their info easily? Our Business Card Reader widget will do just that. Simply drag the widget onto your canvas and deploy. Then, use your phone’s camera to take a picture, and our widget will parse the info and save it to your report. 

QR Code Generator

Our QR Code Generator widget creates a QR code from a combination of input fields from your app. It is a great way to transfer captured data to another system that can scan a QR Code to input data. It’s also a great way for two devices running the same SOTI Snap app to transfer information without needing a data connection.

In a situation where one person begins to fill out a form but can’t finish it or needs someone else's input, a second person can scan the first’s QR code and everything captured will appear on the second person's device, all without a data connection.

Global Background Image

You can now pick a background image to use throughout your app, helping you establish your branding or enhance the look and feel of your apps. Upload your own custom images or use some of the great-looking ones we included to get you started.

Device Scaling

Bring a what-you-see-is-what-you-get editing experience to your SOTI Snap app development. If you know the device you are deploying to, you can enable device scaling on the canvas to see exactly how it will look on a real device, ensuring you know exactly how your custom app will look when it goes live.

PDF Mapper Eraser

Including a picture as part of a submitted PDF is a great way to reduce the need to enter long comments or notes to describe a mitigative action or problem. But what if you didn’t want to redesign the PDF template from scratch? The new PDF Mapper Eraser can be used to remove the areas of the PDF you aren’t using and place the pictures that provide all the necessary details in the blank area. The eraser can even color-match the PDF’s background or field, keeping everything consistent.

General Improvements

The following improvements have been made with this release:

- Administrators can now enable PIN resets for an agent device.

- SOTI Snap allows users to duplicate existing Data Connection rules and retain field mappings from the original rule.

- You can now toggle features at the tenant level and pick and choose features for individual tenants.

- SOTI Snap IDE shows a performance warning message when an administrator tries to enter more than 100 input widgets in a form.

- We removed a restriction that required only alphanumeric characters when renaming a tenant.

- We added a Refresh button to Apps so you can see and access apps as soon as they are created.

- Unique name for widgets, pages, forms, hamburger menu, webpage & image hotspot in an app.

For any questions on new features, please email us at support@soti.net

Sincerely,
Your Product Management Team (SOTI Snap)

&copy; 2023 SOTI Inc. All Rights Reserved.

# SOTI Snap article — Upcoming release of SOTI Snap (Version 4.1)
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001n4C9F2
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2023-03-10

Publish Date: 10-Mar-2023

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

We are happy to inform you that SOTI Snap 4.1 is all set for release this month.

Here is a quick list of key features that will be available in the new

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

We are happy to inform you that SOTI Snap 4.1 is all set for release this month.

Here is a quick list of key features that will be available in the new version:

- Custom PDF Templates: Our new custom PDF template function can map data captured in a Snap app to fill out a PDF document. This is especially useful when your organization has existing PDF forms as it eliminates the errors and inefficiencies of forms filled in with a pen or pencil. After submitting the form data, the perfectly formatted PDF can be emailed out to specified addresses or archived to an online repository like SharePoint, Google Drive, or even an FTP .

- Carousel Widget: The Carousel widget lets you display pages of images or information that can be hosted in a rotating set of panes. Users can swipe left or right to the next pane, or have it automatically jump to the next pane after a set time interval. The carousel can be very helpful for displaying a defined template with different information like displaying a select inventory of parts to choose from, or introducing a set of event speakers.

- Set Focus Action and On Enter Event: We’ve introduced the Set Focus action and On Enter event to help improve app ease of use. With the On Enter event, clicking enter on the keyboard will move the user to the next field, making filling in longer forms that much easier. The Set Focus action can be coupled with events like On Enter to move focus to another field or even a different page, helping the user visually move from one step to the next.

- Workflow Approval Management: Changes to the Workflow Approval feature now mean administrators can assign any pending approvals to new approvers. If an approver is no longer available for any reason, this handy feature allows administrator to transfer all those pending approvals under the old approver to a new approver without requiring a new workflow.

- Search Workflow Records on Agent: Users with the device agent can now search for any workflow items from their device using either the workflow ID or submitter name. Please note that the agent must be updated before this feature is available.

- Show/Hide Workflow Widget Label: You can now manage the available space in the workflow listing screen with the ability to show or hide a label’s value in the Inbox and My Request pages.

- Keyboard Shortcuts: With SOTI Snap 4.1, we have added more keyboard shortcuts to save time and effort when creating apps.

- Ctrl + C -> Copy selected widget/workflow activity

- Ctrl + V -> Paste selected widget/workflow activity

- Ctrl + Z -> Undo the last done activity

- Ctrl + Y -> Redo the last undone activity

- Ctrl + B -> Bold, applicable for widgets that support text formatting

- Ctrl + I -> Italics, applicable for widgets that support text formatting

- Ctrl + U -> Underline, applicable for widgets that support text formatting

- Delete key -> Deletes the selected widget/workflow activity

-

- Blockly Improvements:

- Hamburger Widget Value Block

- Get hamburger menu name – obtains the name for the hamburger menu

- Set hamburger name – set a text or string to be displayed as the name

- Get hamburger menu image – obtains the image for the hamburger menu

- Set hamburger menu image – set an image to be displayed in the hamburger menu

-

- Set Focus – Enable user to set focus to their desired widget

- Map Widget Value Block

- Add marker – add a marker on the map with the respective latitude and longitude

- Remove marker – remove selected marker from the map

- Set zoom – zoom in to map

- General Improvements: The following improvements have been made with this release:

- Report downloading has been improved with custom and default templates

- Textbox validation can been set to include a range of text

- Minimum, maximum, and initial dates can be set to include range of date

For any questions on new features, please email us at support@soti.net

Sincerely yours,

Product Management Team (SOTI Snap)

&copy; 2023 SOTI Inc. All rights reserved.

# SOTI Snap article — Upcoming release of SOTI Snap (Version 4.0)
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001n629FB
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2022-11-09

Publish Date: 09-Nov-2022

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

We are happy to inform you that SOTI Snap 4.0 is all set for release this month.

Here is a quick list of key features that will be available in the new version:

-

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

We are happy to inform you that SOTI Snap 4.0 is all set for release this month.

Here is a quick list of key features that will be available in the new version:

- Workflow: Drag and drop workflow process builder that gives anyone the power to easily automate and map approval-based business processes across departments in a SNAP. Workflow apps with complete audit trails help any company to streamline approval processes, identify inefficiencies, increase transparency, and improve compliance.

- Schedule Reports: SOTI Snap brings an efficient way to track reports on a periodic basis. App developers can schedule a desired report (say, daily, weekly, monthly basis) for intended console users.

- Granular app permissions: App owners can now avoid unintended actions (such app edits, report access) from their app collaborators. SOTI Snap enables app owners to exercise granular control over the actions performed by their app collaborators.

- Advanced Table Widget: Table Widget now allows App developers to build SOTI Snap apps using which end users can create, update or delete a record in addition to read from a connected private database

- General Improvements

- Report downloading experience has been improved to avoid any download failures

- Customers can download assets (such as images, signature) along with CSV records

- Video Player Widget is no longer a beta feature now. It is available even on iOS devices.

- Textbox now supports regular expressions (regex) for validations

- SOTI Snap data access controller supports stored procedures

- Several blockly improvements have been made by introducing new blocks and enhancing existing blocks.

For any questions on new features, please email us at support@soti.net

Sincerely yours,
Product Management Team (SOTI Snap)

&copy; 2022 SOTI Inc. All rights reserved.

# SOTI Snap article — The Injury Reporting Application by SOTI SNAP
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001n60FC1
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2022-10-11

Publish Date: 11-Oct-2022

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

Create an injury reporting app with SOTI Snap

Workplace injuries are common incidents which can cost both employer and employee valuable time and money. How does your organization handle injuries or incident reports? With SOTI Snap, you can simplify this process and allow you to focus on the importance – taking care of your employees and colleague when they are injured on the job.

Get the app from the SOTI Snap marketplace

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

Create an injury reporting app with SOTI Snap

Workplace injuries are common incidents which can cost both employer and employee valuable time and money. How does your organization handle injuries or incident reports? With SOTI Snap, you can simplify this process and allow you to focus on the importance – taking care of your employees and colleague when they are injured on the job.

Get the app from the SOTI Snap marketplace

Step 1) Go to the SOTI Central > Navigate to Marketplace > Click Mobile Apps > Scroll to look for the injury reporter app.

Figure 1 Getting the App from SOTI Marketplace

Step 2) Click on “Use this App” > Follow the tutorial on how to install a SOTI Snap APP.

Figure 2 Copying token

Step 3) Once complete, the injury reporter will show up in your list of Apps in SOTI Snap.

Figure 3 Injury Reporter App in App Manager

Editing the Splash Page

Open the app to edit. In the project Tab, click on the Splash page, you can edit the page, or remove it.

Step 1) Click Project > Splash page > Replace the background image, if necessary, in page properties.

Figure 4 Navigating the Splash Page

Step 2) To remove the splash page&shy; > Hover mouse over Splash page > Click on trash can icon.

Figure 5 Removing Splash Page

Editing the Main and Submit Page

Next, we can edit the main page(s) of the app. Click on the main Page and select the widget you want to change. Use the properties window in the right to change the elements.

For example, the "employee name" can be displayed as a text in the textbox widget to allow user to understand that’s the box to fill out the injured employee name. There are other settings that you can enable such as a “Required” input field. See Figure 6.

Step 1) Select the textbox widget > Enter text in the Display Text field > Click Required.

Figure 6 Creating Required Textbox

Pro tip: To enhance the visual appearance of the app, a company logo can be added to the top of the page.

Figure 7 Adding in Image Widget for Company Logo

Step 2) Click widgets > Drag and drop the image widget to the canvas > Upload the desired company logo in the properties field.

Figure 8 Inserting Company Logo

Pro tip: Similarly, other pages can be edited in the same manner.

Editing the Triage Page

The triage menu is a selection of guided tutorials on treating injuries such as scrapes and bruises; step by step on how to conduct CPR. 5 preselected videos are provided in the app for reference. To remove or add videos, refer to the following steps.

Step 1) To remove video, click on the layout which house the YouTube video > Select the “trash can” icon to delete.

Figure 9 Deleting video in triage

Step 2) For adding video, click on the layout which house the YouTube video > Select the “copy” icon to copy the layout (left of the trash icon in Figure 9) > Edit the video using the YouTube Properties by clicking on the edit icon as shown in Figure 10 > Navigate to YouTube to get the desire URL or search for the video as shown in Figure 11.

Pro tip: You can also use CTRL+C and CTRL+V to copy and paste.

Figure 10 Copying the YouTube Video Layout

Figure 11 Inserting YouTube URL

Step 3) Click on the paragraph widget > Edit display text to better describe the video.

Figure 12 Editing the Description for the Triage Video

Step 4) Re-Publish the app with the publish icon in the top right.

Figure 13 Re-Publish the App

Step 5) On device > Launch Snap > Swipe down on the main page to install/refresh the apps.

Preview of the Injury Report App

This preview demonstrates how to use the injury reporting app.

1) Enter the fields for the injured employee and other info.

Figure 14 Filling in the preliminary fields

2) Click on the injured body part, see Figure 15.

Figure 15 Selecting Injured Body Parts

3) For smaller regions, you can click on the body part’s name as well. On the next page, you can provide additional information in the injury notes and add in pictures using the camera.

Figure 16 Illustrating the injury

4) Click the “Simple Triage” button to watch videos.

Figure 17 Navigating the simple triage page

5) Click Submit to save the data when done

Figure 18 Submitting the form

# SOTI Snap article — SOTI SNAP Video Training Application Part 2
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001nE126B
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2022-09-22

Publish Date: 22-Sep-2022

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

PART 2 – Summary and analytics

Create a summary page

We need to create a separate “Summary Page” that sits outside of the Form, but it’s still part of the app. You’ll need the following widget:

- Label – List out the summary elements

Step 1) Open your app > go to the Project Tab.

Step 2) On the left panel’s top right corner, click on the

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

PART 2 – Summary and analytics

Create a summary page

We need to create a separate “Summary Page” that sits outside of the Form, but it’s still part of the app. You’ll need the following widget:

- Label – List out the summary elements

Step 1) Open your app > go to the Project Tab.

Step 2) On the left panel’s top right corner, click on the + icon > add a page. Name it “Summary Page” or a name of your choice.

Figure 7 Adding in Summary Page

Step 3) Drag 2 label widgets onto your canvas as shown in Figure 8. You'll want to rename ID/Name for easier identification purposes (i.e.: Labelscore and labelvid)

Figure 8 Summary Page Layout

Create global variables

For this app, we will use the global variables feature to store counters to track if the video was watched or not.

Step 1) Click on the Data Connection Rules tab and create 2 Global Variables.

Pro tip: We only add to the global variable count when all the videos of that playlist is completed.

Figure 9 Establishing Global Variables

- For this example:

- Global variable Video1 reflects on number of times the video was played.

- Global variable Question1 reflects on the results from the post video quiz.

Figure 10 Two Established Global Variables

Pro tip: You need a new global variable to track each video player widget you use.

Use Blockly to define the logic

SOTI Blockly is a great and flexible way of making your widgets and app more powerful. Here we will use Blockly to count the number of times the user watched the video. You will need the following blocks:

- Global Variables – Set and Get Block

- Logic – IF Block

Step 1) Click on the video player widget > navigate to Events Tab.

Figure 11 Access Events to Run Blockly Editor

Step 2) Click Events dropdown and select “On Video End” > Click Add action > select Run Blockly > launch the editor.

Figure 12 Setting up Blockly for Video Player Widget

Step 3) Provide a name on the top left of the Blockly Editor > Save > Add.

Step 4) Click Events tab > Select on Video End > Add navigate to next page (Quiz).

Figure 13 Events for Video Player Widget

Note: Ensure the sequence of actions are followed as shown in Figure 13.

Add the logic for the analytics via Blockly (Quiz Questions)

Add the Blockly logic for the quiz questions to track the number of questions answered correctly. You will need the following blocks:

- Logic – IF Block

- Global Variable – Set Block

- Widget Value – Radio buttons, Set To Block

Step 1) Click on the radio buttons from the Quiz Page > click Events tab > run Blockly Editor.

Figure 14 Blockly for Setting Up Quiz Question

Note: This step can be repeated for each unique question.

Pro tip: To map a checkbox widget, replace the radio button block with the checkbox block.

Add the logic for the analytics via Blockly (Summary Page)

The final step is to display the results in the summary page. You will need the following blocks:

- Local Variables – Create variable > Set Block & Individual Local Variables

- Math – 1 + 1 Block

- Global Variables – Get Block

- Widget Value – Labels Set Block

- Text – Text Block and Create Text Block

Step 1) Click on the summary page > access Events > run Blockly Editor.

&shy;&shy;&shy;&shy;&shy;

Figure 15 Blockly for Summary Page

Note: Create two local variables with respect to the Global variables

- "ANS" for Question1 (number of questions scored correctly)

- "Num" for Video1 (number of times video was watched)

- The names for the local variables can be changed as desired

- Make sure you save your Blockly code

Pro tip: If there are more than 1 question > Create a new global variable > Follow the same step as Figure 14 > Create the Blockly as shown in Figure 16.

Figure 16 Blockly for Summary Page (2 Questions)

Linking Part 1 & 2 together

Step 1) Click on Submit button -> Events Tab > add an action to Navigate to the Summary page.

Figure 17 Adding in Sequence of Events for Submit Button

&shy;&shy;Your Video Training app is officially complete. Feel free to reach out to the SOTI Snap team if you have any questions.

https://youtu.be/_RoJAh_pFuo

# SOTI Snap article — SOTI SNAP Video Training Application
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001nAAE39
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2022-08-18

Publish Date: 18-Aug-2022

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

Create a video learning app with SOTI SNAP

A video-based learning system is an effective way of training your users. Custom E-learning software may be expensive and often requires a computer to run. What if you could design a single custom application that can run on both iOS and Android so they can learn virtually anywhere? With SOTI Snap’s video player feature, you can!

Outline of the video training application

Th

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

Create a video learning app with SOTI SNAP

A video-based learning system is an effective way of training your users. Custom E-learning software may be expensive and often requires a computer to run. What if you could design a single custom application that can run on both iOS and Android so they can learn virtually anywhere? With SOTI Snap’s video player feature, you can!

Outline of the video training application

The app will play a video(s) with multiple-choice questions in the follow up page. Next, we’ll use global variables to track played videos and correct answers - displaying the results. Their scores can also be viewed in the SOTI Snap’s reporting console.

Setting up the project

Start with a form-based app with 2 pages, then a separate page to show the results.

Step 1) In the App Manager, click New App > Create App > Pick a Form based app. This template comes with default fields, remove as needed.

Figure 1 Default Fields

Step 2) In the Forms area on the left, hover your mouse over it and click the + button > create a page 2. Rename these if you want.

Figure 2 Adding Pages in the Form

The Video Player widget

Step 1) Click on the Widgets Tab on the left and add the Video Player widget onto the canvas.

Figure 3 Adding in Video Player Widget

Step 2) In the Properties panel on the right, find the Playlist section and start by giving your video a name and then click the pencil edit icon next to it. The path is where you’d place the URL of the video. Here’s an example: ( http://commondatastorage.googleapis.com/gtv-videos bucket/sample/ForBiggerBlazes.mp4 ). Click Save once done.

Figure 4 Adding in Video

Pro tip: Add additional videos to the Playlist by clicking on the Add button.

Step 3) Choose how the video behaves on start. Here the video will auto play in full screen as soon as the page is loaded and in landscape mode.

Figure 5 Player Modes Settings

Publishing this will create an app that will automatically play a full screen landscaped video, without any controls on the screen.

Add questions for your post video questionnaire

On the same form but on page 2, (Project Tab > Page 2) add questions by using the following 3 widgets

- Section – To house and separate each question, it’ll also let us assign a global variable to track whether the answer was correct or not.

- Paragraph – To write questions.

- Radio button or Checkbox – Decide on what the answers will be. Radio button for single choice, checkbox for multiple correct answers.

Step 1) Drag a section widget to the canvas.

Step 2) Add a paragraph widget inside it and type in your question.

Step 3) Add the checkbox or radio button widget below the paragraph for your answers, you can remove the autowrap feature in the properties, so each answer is on its own line.

Pro tip: Rename ID/Name of every radio button/checkbox so you know exactly what this question is in the report. If I am asking for a car colour, I might name that radio button widget as “Car Colour”.

Pro tip: You can duplicate the section and modify the widgets within to add additional questions.

Figure 6 Adding Questions

This concludes part 1 of the video player. To take your app to the next level with analytics, continue to part 2.

# SOTI Snap article — Upcoming changes to SOTI Snap Android and iOS agent Version Support
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001n46D60
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2022-08-03

Publish Date: 03-Aug-2022

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

To take advantage of the latest features, use new apps, or in some cases, avoid data loss on devices, we strongly recommend upgrading SOTI Snap Android and iOS apps to the 3.3.0 version.

What is the impact of this

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

To take advantage of the latest features, use new apps, or in some cases, avoid data loss on devices, we strongly recommend upgrading SOTI Snap Android and iOS apps to the 3.3.0 version.

What is the impact of this change?

Only devices running SOTI Snap 3.3.0 agent or above can take full advantage of all new apps and features available. Any device running SOTI Snap agents below 3.3.0 will not be able to use new apps, new features, and in some cases, older versions of the agent risk losing their local device data as well.

What should customers do to avoid any impact?

To avoid any disruption in business operations, customers are advised to upgrade their SOTI Snap agent to 3.3.0 as soon as possible. Any device not upgraded to 3.3.0 by August End, 2022, will automatically upgraded when they connect to SOTI Snap.

How to upgrade to the latest SOTI Snap version?

You can upgrade your SOTI Snap agent with any of the following methods:

- From the SOTI Snap agent app catalog, go to Settings > About us > App version, then click Update

- Open Google Play (Android) or the App Store (iOS) and search for SOTI Snap, then click Update

- Refresh the SOTI Snap agent app catalog to receive a notification to upgrade and click Upgrade

Using SOTI MobiControl to Deploy SOTI Snap

If you are using SOTI MobiControl to deploy SOTI Snap and device users do not have access to their respective app store to upgrade the app, the upgrade must instead be sent from SOTI MobiControl.

# SOTI Snap article — Fetching data from MobiControl with a Snap App
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001n1087D
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2022-01-27

Publish Date: 27-Jan-2022

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

#### Introduction

Sometimes users want to tightly integrate SOTI Snap and MobiControl. For example, when you need to verify in an app if a particular device is online or when was the last check-in time, etc. There can be various scenarios on why you would want to bring in data from MobiControl into a Snap app depend

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

#### Introduction

Sometimes users want to tightly integrate SOTI Snap and MobiControl. For example, when you need to verify in an app if a particular device is online or when was the last check-in time, etc. There can be various scenarios on why you would want to bring in data from MobiControl into a Snap app depending on your scenario.

In this tutorial we will be showing how to fetch some basic information from a MobiControl instance into a Snap app. We will create a simple application in Snap with two textboxes and a button that, when clicked, will trigger our blockly event to get some information from a MobiControl instance.

#### Prerequisites

- Access to a Snap instance

- Access to a MobiControl instance

- Having MobiControl API credentials

- A Mobile device enrolled to Snap

#### Creating Application in Snap that can Fetch Data from MobiControl

##### Step 1

To get started, go ahead and create a brand-new app. Select the form and, on the properties tab on the right, toggle off Automatic Navigation.

Next, select the submit button that was automatically created when you created the application, navigate to events tap and remove all existing actions.

Rename the labels and the button text of your app accordingly. Here is the example we will be using with two labels, two textboxes and a button :

Now we will be creating a variable to store the API authentication token. To do so, navigate to Data Connections Rule > Global Variables , s elect New Variable , enter “token” for the variable name , and leave retain value toggle d off.

Go back to the project, select the first page of the form, and navigate to the E vents tab on the top right corner. We will add an event to execute on page load. In other words, the event will run every time a user opens this page on the device.

Notice that under Events, “On Load” event is selected. Click on Add button , choose Run Blockly from the list and click Blockly Editor

We will build the API request to get the authentication token from MobiControl. This token will be used in all our subsequent requests to authenticate with MobiControl server.

First, name your blockly script on the top left corner and add the Rest Service block to the canvas.

Change the request method to POST and enter the token URL endpoint like https://server.domain.tld/MobiControl/api/token . Check MobiControl API documentation for more information.

Next enter the Client ID as the username and Client secret as the password in the Authentication tab . (The ClientID and Client secret are provided when you create the MobiControl API credentials. Contact SOTI Support for more details if needed) .

Once you enter the username and password, move to the Data tab of the REST block and add the following properties:

Also, change the data encoding type to x-www-form- urlencoded . This is what it looks like on our sample app. Example:

Now click the test button to test the connection and once you get the response click done.

Add a set global variable block into the on-success section of the RES T block, then expand the response object, search for the token , and assign it to the global variable we created previously.

Lastly, save the script and exit blockly IDE.

##### Step 2

We will now add an event to the button to request the device information. To do this, select the button and navigate to Events > Run Blockly > Blockly Editor . In the editor, create a local variable named “localToken” and assign the content of the token global variable to it, like so:

Next, we will be making an API call to get the device information using the ‘localToken’ variable we previously created. So, add a REST block to the canvas, select GET method, and enter the device information endpoint. The URL follows the following format: https:// your_domain_here /mobicontrol/api/devices/ device_id

Here is my entire blockly script for the button:

Notice two things:

In the request Headers, I added an authorization property with the ‘localToken’ variable preceded by the word “bearer “as a value.

I mapped the DeviceName and LastCheckInTime parameters from the response object to the two textboxes I have available in my application in the on success section of the block.

Once you publish and run the application on the device, you can click on the button to get the Device Name and CheckIn Time to the textboxes in the app.

# SOTI Snap article — Upcoming release of SOTI Snap (Version 3.1.0)
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001nC94C0
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2021-08-06

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

#### SOTI Snap 3.1.0 is all set for release in Aug 2021

Here is a quick list of key features that will be available in the new version:

-
Users and Permissions: Administrators have greater control when assigning console user roles. Roles can be created based on child permissions and can be assigned to users or groups.

-
Save Reports: You can save a report with filter queries and other settings using a highly intuitive new filter component.

-
App-level Data Connections: Management of third-party data connections, such as Google and Microsoft accounts, is now done at the app level rather than the version level.

-
Auto-sync Data Sources: You can set up daily, weekly, or monthly data synchronization schedules to automatically update data sources of the apps (installed on devices) without re-publishing them

-
New Blockly Blocks: You can make a call to Rest API using a new Data connection block.

For any questions on new features, please email us at support@soti.net

Product Management Team (SOTI Snap)

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

#### SOTI Snap 3.0.0 is all set for release on Saturday, February 6

Here is a quick list of key features that will be available in the new version:

- SOTI Snap Data Link: App developers can now export app data to third party systems hosted on any secure network. A new component, Snap Data Link, enables and automates the export of app data directly to Microsoft SQL and Email servers that are not exposed to the internet for security reasons.

- Publish A pps in Test/ Production Environments: App developers can create independent test environments to deploy apps. App versions can be published to any available test or production environment. Device users can access apps in each environment using the SOTI Snap Agent.

- Custom Block s: Blockly has introduced Custom Blocks to offer the most flexibility when building out your apps. Custom Blocks allow SOTI S nap app developers to easily save and reuse logic blocks they have created, within the same app or across multiple apps. Advanced users can define completely new blocks in Blockly by writing their own JavaScript or by incorporating third-party JavaScript code into their projects.

For any questions on new features, please email us at support@soti.net

Product Management Team (SOTI Snap)

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

Starting v2.2.0, SOTI Snap will end support for devices running versions of iOS 9.x and lower.

The SOTI Snap v2.2.0 iOS agent will only work on devices running versions of iOS 10.0 and higher.

Any devices still running iOS 9.x or lower will not be able to install the SOTI Snap v2.2.0 iOS agent.

In addition, devices that have the SOTI Snap v2.1.0 agent will not be able to receive updates of SOTI Snap apps that are published in the SOTI Snap v2.2.0 Web Console.

Those who want to continue using SOTI Snap will need to upgrade to minimum iOS version 10.0.

SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2020-03-23

#### Summary

#### SOTI Snap 2.1 is all set for release later this week on Saturday, March 28!

Here is a quick list of upcoming key features:

- Share your apps 
App developers can now develop apps in one SOTI Snap account and then share them with another SOTI Snap account, vastly reducing the time and effort necessary to rebuild identical or highly similar apps in different environments.

- Control access to your apps
Previously, all apps w

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

#### SOTI Snap 2.1 is all set for release later this week on Saturday, March 28!

Here is a quick list of upcoming key features:

- Share your apps 
App developers can now develop apps in one SOTI Snap account and then share them with another SOTI Snap account, vastly reducing the time and effort necessary to rebuild identical or highly similar apps in different environments.

- Control access to your apps
Previously, all apps were accessible by all users and other users could make changes to apps without consulting the original app developer. App developers can now control who can access or edit their apps.

- SharePoint, OneDrive and FTP/SFTP Data Export rules
SOTI Snap will now support  SharePoint, OneDrive and FTP/SFTP as data export destinations. 

- Improved app loading experience 
All images in a SOTI Snap app are now completely downloaded when a SOTI Snap app is launched. Users can now track the downloading progress of the apps being installed or updated in the App Catalog

- Advanced number formatting   
Numeric formatting now includes options for adding a prefix or suffix (such as currency symbols, for example) or specifying decimal places and number group separators.

Keep a watch on this space for more details.

For any questions on new features, email us at support@soti.net.

Sincerely yours,
SOTI Snap Team

&copy; 2020 SOTI Inc. All rights reserved.

Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/SOTI-Snap/articles/com2OF0000001nC06BF
SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2019-09-10

Publish Date: 10-Sep-2019

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

##### SOTI Snap 2.0 simplifies app creation for business users and enables organizations to deliver the apps their business needs faster.

###### SOTI Snap 2.0 delivers a true no-code approach for rapid app development with the integration of Blockly.

- Blockly is a visual programming editor that makes it easy for non-developers to write advanced logic simply by dragging and dropping

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

##### SOTI Snap 2.0 simplifies app creation for business users and enables organizations to deliver the apps their business needs faster.

###### SOTI Snap 2.0 delivers a true no-code approach for rapid app development with the integration of Blockly.

- Blockly is a visual programming editor that makes it easy for non-developers to write advanced logic simply by dragging and dropping logic blocks onto a web canvas. Using Blockly, business domain experts can create apps that handle complex calculations, custom validations and more to deliver rich functionality to their app users without the need for coding or developers.

###### SOTI Snap 2.0 brings new automation capabilities and app components to create rich enterprise apps.

- Trigger a sequence of actions, including Blockly logic, when an event is triggered in the app, such as the click of a button.

- Build modern apps with easy navigation using a hamburger menu.

- Re-use existing mobile website pages within an app.

- Bring images within a Snap app to life by adding multiple hotspots on different parts of the same image. Configure custom actions on the click of an image hotspot.

- Publish app announcements to send useful information to the app users.

###### SOTI Snap 2.0 helps you scale enterprise mobility across multiple departments

- SOTI Snap allows multiple departments within an organization develop their own apps on allocated Snap channels. And with multiple channels enrollment, device users can access apps published by different departments in the Snap agent.

For any questions on new features, please email us at support@soti.net

Sincerely yours,
SOTI Snap Team

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

Issue: When a User adds the first widget on the screen, there is a default spacing between the header and widget

Affects: UI when a User prefers no spacing between the header and widget

Cause: There is a default top padding (with 12 spaces) for the screen

Resolution: Change the screen padding to Zero to remove the spacing:

- On the Left Navigation pane, select the screen on which you have added the widget. Eg. List Widget

- On the Page Properties panel on the right-hand side, Select ‘Format’ tab.

- Reduce Top Padding to Zero in ‘Margin/Padding’ section.

- The spacing will be removed

SOTI Snap · Pulse article · published 2018-10-16

Publish Date: 16-Oct-2018

SOTI Snap

#### Summary

#### Thank you for participating in the Beta trial of SOTI Snap. Effective November 1 st a new version of SOTI Snap will be released and all SOTI Snap beta accounts and apps will be deleted.

##### Impact on Web Console Users

- Web console users won't be able to access SOTI SNAP with their login credentials

- All apps, associated data, and configurations will be permanently removed from SOTI SNAP

#### Related SOTI ONE Platform Products

SOTI Snap

#### Process Description

#### Thank you for participating in the Beta trial of SOTI Snap. Effective November 1 st a new version of SOTI Snap will be released and all SOTI Snap beta accounts and apps will be deleted.

##### Impact on Web Console Users

- Web console users won't be able to access SOTI SNAP with their login credentials

- All apps, associated data, and configurations will be permanently removed from SOTI SNAP

- Web console users need to be registered again with a fresh SOTI SNAP account

##### Impact on Device Users

- Users will be forced to upgrade their devices for new SOTI SNAP Agent

- Apps and any associated offline data will be permanently removed from the device

- Users need to contact Admin to get their devices re-enrolled via fresh SOTI SNAP account

##### How to take Back-up?

If there is any submitted app data you would like retained, please export your data using app reports or via the SOTI Snap API prior to November 1 st . For assistance on exporting your data, please contact support@soti.net .

##### Sign up for a commercial trial of SOTI ONE – which includes SOTI Snap, SOTI MobiControl, and SOTI Assist – or reach out to a SOTI sales representative ( sales@soti.net ) on November 1 st .

# SOTI Snap help — Using SOTI Snap Device Agent Logs
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/agent_log_view
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/agent_log_view

### Using SOTI Snap Device Agent Logs

#### About this task

To access event logs for SOTI Snap apps, do the following:

#### Procedure

- You can use event logs from the SOTI Snap Device Agent or from the app itself.
- To access from SOTI Snap Device Agent, navigate to the SOTI Snap event logs: App Catalog > > Settings > View Event Logs .

- To access from the app, select > View Logs .

The Event Logs screen displays.

- From the Event Logs screen, you can perform the following available tasks with the corresponding procedures: | | Task | Procedure
| Sending SOTI Snap Device Agent Debug Report to SOTI Snap Administrators | To send SOTI Snap Device Agent debug reports to the SOTI Snap administrators:
- Select > Send Logs .

Results: SOTI Snap Device Agent sends event logs to your SOTI Snap administrator.
| Sharing SOTI Snap Device Agent Logs | To send event logs for the SOTI Snap Device Agent using email:
- Select > Share . The device's default email app opens.

- Enter email addresses in the To field. The Subject and Body fields are pre-populated with text but you can change them to suit your needs.

- Send the email.

Results: SOTI Snap Device Agent sends an email with the attached event logs (as a .txt file) to the email addresses you specified.
| Downloading SOTI Snap Device Agent Logs | To download event logs for the SOTI Snap Device Agent:
- Select > Download .

Results: SOTI Snap Device Agent downloads the event logs to your device as a .txt file.

# SOTI Snap help — SOTI Snap Agent Help
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/appuser
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/appuser

### SOTI Snap Agent Help

Gain access to custom apps built by your organization.
The SOTI Snap agent collects all of the apps that your organization built in SOTI Snap and published for your use. Once you install and log into the SOTI Snap agent, you'll have immediate access to all your company apps from a central location.
Each app is autonomous. Although they were created by the same organization, the apps do not share data.
Download the SOTI Snap agent from the Apple App Store or the Google Play store.
Note: Your organization must create and publish a SOTI Snap app before you can use the SOTI Snap agent.

# SOTI Snap help — Cycle through Multiple Apps
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/concurrent_apps
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/concurrent_apps

### Cycle through Multiple Apps

#### About this task

To open multiple apps in the SOTI Snap agents and easily switch between them:

#### Procedure

- Open an app from the SOTI Snap agent app catalog.

- Tap Tabs in the top-right to minimize the current app and then tap New App .

- From the app catalog, tap another app to open it.

- Repeat for any other apps you want to open.

#### Results

You can cycle between apps by tapping Tabs to see all open apps, then swiping left or right to scroll through the open apps. On Android devices, each app also appears as a separate entry in the Recent Apps screen.
Note: While inside the SOTI Snap agent, you can only cycle between apps within the same channel.
To close an app, tap Tabs and swipe up from the bottom to dismiss it.
To close all apps, tap Tabs and then tap Exit .

# SOTI Snap help — Constructing an API URL for the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/construct_an_api_url_for_dac
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/construct_an_api_url_for_dac

### Constructing an API URL for the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller

Use the API Docs tab to view the API documentation and construct client URL (cURL) requests to access API endpoints for your SOTI Snap Data Access Controller (DAC) services. You can also use the Construct URL tool to have the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller construct cURL requests.

#### Before you begin

The API documentation explains how to connect to your SOTI Snap DAC services for executing creating, reading, updating and deleting (CRUD) operations from your database tables. Important: The cURL request examples in this section and the Construct URL tool follow standard cURL request format rules and run using a Bash shell. The request examples may lead to unexpected behaviour when using Windows Command Prompt or Windows PowerShell, which follow different character escaping rules.

#### About this task

To construct a cURL request using the Construct URL tool:

#### Procedure

- Log into the SOTI Snap DAC web console. Then, select the API Docs tab.

- In the top-right corner of the API Docs page, select Construct URL .

- Choose a SOTI Snap DAC service for which you would like to construct a cURL request. Select Next . Note: In the screenshot above, TestSQLConnection is an example service. When chosen, the constructed cURL request uses TestSQLConnection 's connection details.

- Select an HTTP request type.
- GET

- POST

- PATCH

- PUT

- DELETE

- STORED PROCEDURE

- CUSTOM QUERY

- Refer to the following table for the instructions associated with each HTTP request type. | | Request | Procedure
| GET |
- Choose the table on which to execute the request. Choose from:
- Retrieve All Records

- Retrieve Single Row: Enter the respective table row's primary key.

- Search, Filtering and Sorting: Enter the following (as required):
- The values for filtering the table's columns

- The number of results per page

- The page number

- The column to use to sort the table
| - POST

- PATCH

- PUT

- DELETE
| - Choose the table on which to execute the request.

- Enter data for the fields of the table.
| STORED PROCEDURE |
- Choose the procedure to execute.

- Enter the values for the respective parameters.
| CUSTOM QUERY | Restriction: This HTTP request is only available if the chosen DAC service allows custom SELECT queries.

- In the text body, enter the custom query.

- Select Construct API URL . The API URL dialog box displays the constructed cURL request under Example of usage . Important: The password in the constructed cURL request remains hidden for security purposes. Replace the asterisks in the password header with the actual password for the service before executing the request.

- Select OK to close the API URL dialog box.

Related information
- SOTI Snap Data Access Controller (DAC)

- Setting up the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller

- Creating a New Service for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller

- Importing an SSL Certificate for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller

- Viewing the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller Request History

- Managing SOTI Snap Data Access Controller Settings

- Connecting SOTI Snap Apps to the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller Service

# SOTI Snap help — Creating a New Service for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/creating_a_new_service
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/creating_a_new_service

### Creating a New Service for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller

#### About this task

Use this procedure to create a RESTful API service by connecting to an existing database. SOTI Snap applications can then perform create, read, update and delete (CRUD) operations on database table data using the API.

#### Procedure

- From the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller web console, select the Services tab.

- From the Services tab, select New Service .

- Select the Database Server Type.
Select a database server type from the drop-down menu:
- PostgreSQL

- MySQL

- Oracle

- Microsoft SQL Server

- IBM Db2

Note: If you wish to connect to a remote Oracle database and encounter errors, you may need to install Oracle Instant Client. Instructions are available at http://127.0.0.1:<console_port>/oracleSetupDocs .

- Set up the Connection to the database server.

- Database Hostname / IP: The hostname/network address that has the database server.

- Port: The port number for accessing the database server on the hostname/network address.

- Username: The username for accessing the database server.

- Password: The database username respective password.

- Select Test Connection to verify that you can successfully connect to the database server.

Important: It is recommended to use a non-administrative account for connecting to the database. The account should be granted appropriate permissions in the database for its intended use.

- If you can successfully connect to the database server, continue to set up the Connection by selecting a database and / or schema.

- Database: Choose a database from the database server.

- Database Schema: Choose a schema from the database you have chosen.

- Select the Tables in the database server to receive API endpoints.

- Optional: Enable Allow for custom SELECT queries to display tables with composite keys and / or no primary key.

- For the Table Name , choose the tables you would like to Create API Endpoint and / or Allow for Custom SELECT Queries .

See the Custom SELECT Queries section in the API Docs tab for more details about allowing custom SELECT queries.
Note: Tables with composite keys and / or no primary key are only accessible using custom SELECT queries.

- Under Routines , select the stored procedures and functions that should be accessible via the API endpoints.

- For Procedure Name , choose the stored procedures that the API endpoint can access.

- For Function Name , choose the functions that custom SELECT queries can access.

See the Stored Procedures section in the API Docs tab for more details about accessing stored procedures and functions.
Restriction: You must choose at least one table or procedure to create an API endpoint for.

- Set up the Authentication details to access the API endpoints.

- Service Name: The name for the RESTful API service.

- Port Number To Host the Service: The port number where the RESTful API service binds to and listens for incoming requests. SOTI Snap applications use this port to connect to the service. By default this value is 5000. Restriction: You must enter a port number from 5000 to 5998, inclusive. No two DAC services can use the same port.

- Username : Enter an alphanumeric string as the API endpoint user.

- Password and Re-enter Password : Enter a password as specified by the API Password Complexity settings (see Managing SOTI Snap Data Access Controller Settings ).

- Optional: Toggle Enable HTTPS to restrict access to HTTPS only for SOTI Snap applications that access this API. This offers a more secure connection.

- In the Summary step, preview the settings for the service. Select Back to make changes. You can also edit your selections and choices after you create the service as needed.

- Select Create Service to create the service.

#### Results

SOTI Snap Data Access Controller adds a new created service row to the Services panel.

- Importing an SSL Certificate for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller

- Viewing the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller Request History

- Constructing an API URL for the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller

# SOTI Snap help — SOTI Snap Data Access Controller (DAC)
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/dac_overview
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/dac_overview

### SOTI Snap Data Access Controller (DAC)

The SOTI Snap Data Access Controller (DAC) is a RESTful API endpoint generator. SOTI Snap DAC applications can perform create, read, update and delete (CRUD) operations to retrieve and submit data directly to your databases.
Some key points about the DAC:

- Functionality : Enables applications to perform CRUD operations to retrieve and submit data directly to databases.

- Supported Databases : PostgreSQL, MySQL, Oracle, Microsoft SQL Server, IBM Db2 Restriction: Microsoft SQL Server 2025 is currently not supported.

- Installation Locations : On the server where your database resides, a dedicated machine, or a remote server within your company, including servers in a demilitarized zone (DMZ).

- Setup : Download the SOTI Snap DAC setup wizard, install it on a Windows server, and accessing it via a web browser.

- Security : It enables secure, direct communication between private databases and SOTI Snap applications via the locally hosted DAC, helping developers meet compliance and security requirements.

Install the SOTI Snap DAC in the following locations:
- The server where your database resides. This server can be a dedicated machine.

- A remote server in your company, including servers in a demilitarized zone (DMZ).

The SOTI Snap DAC supports the following databases:
- PostgreSQL

- MySQL

- Oracle

- IBM Db2

- Microsoft SQL Server Restriction: Microsoft SQL Server 2025 is currently not supported.

CAUTION: Third-party applications with valid credentials can access RESTful API endpoints generated by SOTI Snap Data Access Controller . SOTI strongly advises against this practice due to the potential risk of credential exposure.

# SOTI Snap help — Enrolling and Managing Channels
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/enroll_multiple
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/enroll_multiple

### Enrolling and Managing Channels

#### Before you begin

Note: You should have received enrollment details from your SOTI Snap administrator, likely via email. If not, contact your SOTI Snap administrator before you begin.

#### About this task

You may need to add another channel to your SOTI Snap Device Agent. A channel is any source from whom you receive SOTI Snap built apps. This can happen if your main company employs a third-party or managed service provider (MSP) to offer other services and that MSP also uses SOTI Snap to create custom apps.
Although you can access apps from different channels from within the same SOTI Snap Device Agent, those apps are entirely separate and do not exchange data with each other.
To add another organization's apps to your current SOTI Snap agent:

#### Procedure

- Open the SOTI Snap Device Agent on your device and tap More > Manage Channels .

- For the following available tasks, select the corresponding respective procedure: | | Task | Procedure
| Enrolling in a Channel |
- Have your enrollment details nearby.

- Select Add to open the SOTI Snap authentication screen.

- Depending on how your SOTI Snap administrator chose to configure enrollment , you need to do one of the following:
- enter your email address

- enter an enrollment ID

- scan a QR code

- Optional: Set up a PIN to increase security for the SOTI Snap Device Agent.
| Rearranging Channel Order | To rearrange the order in which channels appear in your SOTI Snap Device Agent:
- Select and hold a channel to emphasize it, then drag it up or down to place it higher or lower in the list of channels. Channels higher in the list appear first in the SOTI Snap Device Agent.

- Repeat with the rest of the channels until you arranged them to your liking.

- Select your back button to return to the main SOTI Snap Device Agent channel screen.
| Removing Channel | Important: If you remove this channel, you lose all data associated with this channel. While you can re-enroll in this channel later, the SOTI Snap Device Agent does not keep any data from the past. To remove a channel from your SOTI Snap Device Agent:
- Select the red minus icon beside the channel you want to remove from the SOTI Snap app. Confirm the removal. Restriction: You cannot remove the last channel.

- Select your back button to return to the main SOTI Snap Device Agent channel screen.

#### Results

You are now enrolled in another SOTI Snap environment. Swipe horizontally in the Device Agent to switch between companies.

SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/enroll

### Enroll in SOTI Snap

#### Before you begin

Note: You should have received enrollment details from your SOTI Snap administrator, likely through email. If not, contact your SOTI Snap administrator before you begin.

#### About this task

The SOTI Snap agent is the central location for accessing your organization's custom built apps.
To gain access to your organization's apps:

#### Procedure

- Have your enrollment details nearby.

- Download the SOTI Snap agent from the Google Play Store or the Apple iTunes Store .

- Depending on how your SOTI Snap administrator chose to configure enrollment, you will need to either enter your email address, enter an enrollment ID, or scan a QR code.

- Optional: Set up a PIN to increase security for the SOTI Snap agent on your device.

#### Results

Apps from your company will immediately begin to download.

# SOTI Snap help — Importing an SSL Certificate for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/import_a_ssl_certificate_for_dac
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/import_a_ssl_certificate_for_dac

### Importing an SSL Certificate for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller

#### Before you begin

Tip: In a production environment, SOTI recommends enabling HTTPS to secure your data.

#### About this task

Upload an SSL certificate to the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller (DAC) to use the more secure HTTPS protocol for an added layer of encryption and verification. Use this procedure to add a SSL Certificate to the SOTI Snap DAC.
By default, the SOTI Snap DAC uses an insecure HTTP connection between the SOTI Snap application and its SOTI Snap DAC endpoints. This sends plain text messages that are vulnerable to security threats. You can upload an SSL certificate to use the more secure HTTPS protocol for an added layer of encryption and verification.

#### Procedure

- Log into the SOTI Snap DAC web console. Then, select the Certificate tab.

- In the Certificate tab, upload the SSL certificate: Important: You must upload both a certificate file and a key file.
Restriction: Encrypted key files aren't supported.

- For Add Certificate , select Choose File and navigate to the certificate file. It's strongly recommended to use a certificate signed by a certificate authority. The certificate must be of type .der , .pem , .crt , or .cer .

- For Add Key , select Choose File and navigate to the key file. The key file must be .key or .pem .

- Optional: For Add Chain File , if the SSL certificate came with a chain file, select Choose File and navigate to the chain file. The chain file must be .pem , .crt , or .cer .

- Select Load Certificate . If the certificate is successfully uploaded, the following information appears:
- Issuer

- Expiration date

- Common name

- Thumbprint

- Select Save Certificate .

#### Results

The SOTI Snap DAC saves the SSL certificate, enabling you to use HTTPS when creating or editing a DAC service

#### What to do next

Proceed with Creating a New Service for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller to create a new service with the saved SSL certificate.

### Managing SOTI Snap Data Access Controller Services

After creating a new service for SOTI Snap DAC, (see Creating a New Service for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller ), you can view information about it.

- Service Name: The display name of the service.

- API Port: The port that your API endpoint clients must use to connect to the service.

- Database Name: The name of the connected database.

- Schema Name: The schema of the connected database.

- Status: Whether the API is enabled.

From the user interface, you can perform the following operations:
- Select New Service to create a new service.

- For a Service Name 's row, you can:
- Toggle the Status switch of the service to enable/disable it.

- Select to open a menu:
- Edit : Edit the settings for the service, such as the database connection, activated tables/routines, display name, port, authentication credentials, and HTTPS configuration (see Creating a New Service for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller ).

- Construct Request URL : see Constructing an API URL for the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller .

- Delete : Delete the service. You need the API endpoint's username and password to confirm the deletion.

### Managing SOTI Snap Data Access Controller Settings

Customize settings of your SOTI Snap Data Access Controller (DAC).

- From the Settings tab's Security sub-tab, you can manage the API password complexity required when creating or editing DAC services.

You can configure the following:
- The minimum number of characters the password must have.

- If the password must have at least one digit.

- If the password must have at least one upper-case letter.

- If the password must have at least one lower-case letter.

- If the password must have at least one special character ( #?!@$^&*- ).

- After configuring the API password complexity, select Save .

- From the Settings tab's Driver sub-tab, you can change the ODBC driver used for connecting to Microsoft SQL Server databases. If the default driver cannot connect successfully, choose another ODBC driver for connecting to Microsoft SQL Server databases when creating a new SOTI Snap DAC service (see Creating a New Service for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller ):

- Choose the driver and select Save .

# SOTI Snap help — Setting up the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/setup_dac
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/setup_dac

### Setting up the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller

To set up the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller (DAC), you must download the setup wizard, install the SOTI Snap DAC, and then access it from your local machine using a web browser.

#### Before you begin

The minimum requirements to install DAC on a virtual machine are:
- OS: Windows Server 2019 / Windows 10 or above

- RAM: 4 Gb

- Disk: 1 GB of free space

- CPU: 2 Cores

#### Step 1: Download the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller Setup Wizard

##### About this task

To download the SOTI Snap DAC setup wizard, do the following:

##### Procedure

- Log into SOTI Snap and do one of the following:
- From the left toolbar, select Settings . Then, from the Settings view, select the SOTI Snap DAC tab.

- In the upper-right corner of the SOTI Snap console, select the drop-down beside your name and select Download Data Access Controller .

- Optional: Select the instructional videos to learn more about the SOTI Snap DAC.

- Select Download .

##### Results

The SOTI Snap DAC setup wizard ( SnapDACInstaller.msi ) is downloaded to your local machine.

##### What to do next

If your local machine is different from your database server, SOTI recommends to move the SOTI Snap DAC setup wizard ( SnapDACInstaller.msi ) to the database server.

#### Step 2: Install SOTI Snap Data Access Controller on a Database Server

##### Before you begin

- The SOTI Snap DAC is only supported on Windows.

- You must have administrator privileges to install the SOTI Snap DAC.

- You must install SOTI Snap DAC on the same drive as the Windows operating system ( C:\ )

- To install the SOTI Snap DAC on a virtual machine, you must satisfy the following minimum requirements:
- Operating System: Windows Server 2019 / Windows 10 or later

- RAM: 4 GB

- Disk: 1 GB of free space

- CPU: 2 Cores

##### About this task

To install the SOTI Snap DAC on the server:

##### Procedure

- Run the SnapDACInstaller.msi and select Next .

- Read the End User License Agreement. Accept the license agreement and select Next .

- Accept the default installation location, or select Browse and choose a folder to install the SOTI Snap DAC. Select Next . Note: The default installation folder is C:\Program Files\SOTI\DAC .
Remember: You must install SOTI Snap DAC on the same drive as the Windows operating system ( C:\ )

- Enter a port number for the DAC Console Port and the DAC Server Port then select Next . Restriction:
- These two port numbers must be different from each other.

- These two port numbers must be within the range of 1024—65535.

- The ports must not currently be in use.

- View the required Dependencies to use SOTI Snap DAC. Select Install to automatically install the required dependencies and SOTI Snap DAC.

- When the installation completes, copy the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller URL. Then, select Finish . Note: The SOTI Snap DAC is accessible at http://127.0.0.1:#####, where ##### is the DAC Port entered earlier.

#### Step 3: Access the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller Web Console

##### About this task

To access the SOTI Snap DAC web console:

##### Procedure

- Open a web browser from the server machine where you installed the SOTI Snap DAC. In the URL bar, paste the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller URL. The Account Creation panel displays. Restriction: You cannot access the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller URL from any other machine.

- Enter the following:
- Email

- Password

- Confirm Password

- Select Create & Login to log into the SOTI Snap DAC web console.

- Optional: Select Take Me There to configure HTTPS (see Importing an SSL Certificate for SOTI Snap Data Access Controller ).

- Select I Understand The Risk .

##### Results

You are now logged into, and have access to, the SOTI Snap DAC web console.

##### What to do next

To log into the web console again, use the following credentials:
- Username: admin .

- Password: The password you entered in the Account Creation panel.

# SOTI Snap help — Troubleshoot SOTI Snap Device Agent Issues
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/app_user/troubleshoot_agent
SOTI Snap · help documentation · app_user/troubleshoot_agent

### Troubleshoot SOTI Snap Device Agent Issues

If you experience issues with your SOTI Snap agent or apps, you may be able to address the issue yourself.
If you can't fix the problem yourself, in the SOTI Snap agent, go to Settings > Help and Support for contact information for your SOTI Snap administrator.
| | Issue | Possible Cause | Fix
| Low Memory warning | Your device storage is too full to receive updates for your SOTI Snap apps. | Free up space in your device storage.
| The SOTI Snap agent won't download the latest app versions | The SOTI Snap agent on your device may be older than the SOTI Snap server that's distributing the apps. | Update your SOTI Snap agent.
