# SOTI Snap help — Configuring Role Management for Business App Components
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/access_control/access_management_for_business_apps
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/access_control/access_management_for_business_apps

### Configuring Role Management for Business App Components

#### About this task

You can selectively configure app components to make them visible or not visible to different device users or device user groups. The Access Management feature enables you to create apps that have different components visible for different users based on the user's granted permissions. The Access Management feature applies to all App Components :
- Form

- Workflow

- Page

- Webpage

- Hamburger Menu (specifically, each hamburger menu item)

- AI Form Builder

App components can be configured to be public or private. In the case of hamburger menu items, enable or disable the need for authentication.
- Public app components and hamburger menu items not needing authentication are visible to all device users.
- Hamburger menu items needing authentication are visible to all authenticated device users.

- Private components and hamburger menu items needing authentication with at least one assigned role are only visible to device users with the appropriately granted permissions.

You can control which users can access specific app components by configuring device access roles for device users or device user groups. Also, you can combine multiple app component permissions. Use the following steps to configure the app component permissions:
- Initialize a New App Component

- Configure the App Component's Visibility

- (Optional) Create/Modify the Role(s) That Can Access the App Component

- Toggle the Roles That Can Access the App Component

#### Step 1: Initialize a New App Component

##### Before you begin

You can configure multiple app components. You must later configure each of the app component's visibility. Then depending on the visibility, you must customize the roles that have visibility to the component.

##### Procedure

- Add a new app component .

- Once you add an app component, you can see a menu for the Access Management feature. | | App Component | Procedure
| - Form

- Workflow

- Page

- Webpage

- AI Form Builder
| Note: When you create a new app component, it is initially by default Private (see Step 2 for more details) with an Admin role assigned to it. This means only device users with an Admin role have visibility to the app component (see Step 3 for more details).

- The Tag field autopopulates with the app component's Name field. You may change this value without automatically changing the Name field. This value helps you identify this app component in Step 2 .

- The Roles field autopopulates with Admin . This field's value determines the roles (its device users and device user groups) that has visibility to this app component. For more details (such as adding other roles), see Step 3 .

- Select the icon to access Device Access 's Access Management panel. For more details, see Step 2 .

#### Step 2: Configure the App Component's Visibility

##### About this task

Set up the app component's visibility. Depending on the visibility you set, you may need to create a new role. | | App Component | Procedure
| - Form

- Workflow

- Page

- Webpage

- AI Form Builder
| See Configuring Access Management (Device Access) For Business Apps Components for details on how to configure the app's access type.

- If you configured the app's access type as Public for this app component, then it is visible to all device users. The app component's configuration is complete.

- If you configured the app's access type as Private for this app component, then the app component is selectively visible to certain roles. Proceed to the next step.
| Hamburger Menu |
- For the hamburger Menu Properties ' Setting section's Access Control subsection, select the icon to access the Menu Item Access Control panel.

- In the Menu Item Access Control panel, you can toggle Authentication for each item.
- If you toggle Authentication off, then the menu item is visible for all device users, and the menu item's configuration is complete.

- If you toggle Authentication on, and you did not enable any roles using Assign Roles , then the menu item is visible to all device users that are successfully authenticated to the SOTI Snap Device Agent. To make the menu item selectively visible to certain roles, proceed to the next step.

#### Step 3: (Optional) Create/Modify the Role(s) That Can Access the App Component

##### About this task

In this optional step, you can create or edit an existing role specifically tailored to access the app component you just created. See Creating a New Role for more information.
If you skip this step, you can only assign or unassign the default Admin role.

#### Step 4: Toggle the Roles That Can Access the App Component

##### About this task

Complete the app component customization by assigning all the roles that has visibility to the app component. | | App Component | Procedure
| - Form

- Workflow

- Page

- Webpage

- AI Form Builder
| See Configuring Access Management (Device Access) For Business Apps Components for details on how to configure the private app component's roles.
| Hamburger Menu | For a hamburger menu item that has Authentication enabled, select Assign Role to toggle all the roles that have selective visibility. If you assign at least one role, then the hamburger menu item becomes only selectively visible to authenticated device users with the assigned roles.

Related information
- Configuring Access Management (Device Access) For Business Apps Components

- Managing Role Administration (Device Access) for Business Apps

# SOTI Snap help — Creating and Managing Users Within Roles
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/access_control/add_and_remove_users
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/access_control/add_and_remove_users

### Creating and Managing Users Within Roles

#### Before you begin

To assign or remove users from roles, you must have Manage Access rights. Members of the Administrators and Co-owners roles have these rights granted by default. You can also grant Manage Access rights under a custom role.

#### About this task

SOTI Snap simplifies the process of adding, changing, or removing users within an app role.
Note: The following activities are the same whether adding a user to a default or custom role. Adding or removing users within roles affects the apps available to them under the Shared With Me tab of the App Manager .

#### Procedure

- Open the Apps view, and beside the published app whose role memberships you want to update, select More > Manage Access .

- For the available tasks, follow the corresponding procedure: | | Task | Procedure
| Adding a User to a Role | To add one or more users to a role:

- In the Console Access tab, select Roles .

- Select to expand the dropdown list beside the role category ( Default or Custom ).

- Pick the name of the role you wish to add a user to, and select Users . Members of the role appear in the panel to the right.

- Select the Assign User button. The Assign Users window opens.

- In the Users section, select the Users search field. Enter the name of the user(s) you wish to add.

- Expand the Roles dropdown list. By default, SOTI Snap selects the requested role and you can select even more roles.

- Select all required roles and then select Done . Note: The rights from each selected role combine with those of the other selected roles. Make sure the role combinations do not result in excessive access.

- Select Add to complete the role assignment.

Results: The added users are in the Users list for the role.
| Changing User Role Memberships | To change a user's role memberships:

- In the Console Access tab, select Users .

- Beside the user's name, select More and then select Assign Role . The Assign Roles window opens.

- From the list of available default and custom roles, select or deselect all applicable roles for the user. Select Save . Note: The rights from each selected role combine with those of the other selected roles.

Results: The user is a member of all selected groups.
| Removing a User from a Role | To remove one or more users from a role:

- In the Console Access tab, select Roles .

- Select to expand the dropdown list beside the role category ( Default or Custom ).

- Select the name of the role you wish to remove a user from, and select Users . Members of the role appear in the panel to the right.

- Beside the name of the user to removed, select More and then select Remove User .

- In the Remove User(s)? confirmation window, select Remove .

Results: You removed the user from the list.

# SOTI Snap help — Managing Default Roles
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/access_control/assign_default_roles
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/access_control/assign_default_roles

### Managing Default Roles

App access management provides three default roles you can assign users to:

- Administrator—Administrators have full rights to manage the app.

- Co-owner—Co-owners have rights to all functions except to delete the app.

- Viewer—Viewers may only duplicate, preview, or examine app versions.

Note: You can not change access rights assigned to these default roles can not and you can not delete default roles. If you require other rights, create custom roles and assign users to them.

Related information
- Creating and Managing Custom App Roles

# SOTI Snap help — Configuring Access Management (Device Access) For Business Apps Components
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/access_control/configuring_access_management_for_business_apps
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/access_control/configuring_access_management_for_business_apps

### Configuring Access Management (Device Access) For Business Apps Components

For business apps, each app component can be selectively configured to be visible for all device users or only for certain roles (specific device users or device user groups). You can access this configuration by selecting Manage Access on a business app (from the Apps > My Apps tab). Alternatively, you can also access this configuration from the Properties ' Access Management section of the app component and selecting the icon.

#### About this task

Remember: In the diagram below, app components are referred to as App Elements .

Important: When you change the Access Type or Role of an app component, it is immediately reflected in the device's SOTI Snap Device Agent.

#### Procedure

- For an app component, select either Make Public or Make Private to set its visibility.
- Select Make Public to configure this app component to be visible to all device users.

- Select Make Private to configure this app component to be visible to only certain roles (specific device users or device user groups). See Managing Role Administration (Device Access) for Business Apps for more information.

- For a Private app component, toggle the Roles that you want to give visibility to. Restriction: An app component must have at least one role that has visibility to it.

- Select All , Private , or Public to filter for app components.

Related information
- Managing Role Administration (Device Access) for Business Apps

- Configuring Role Management for Business App Components

# SOTI Snap help — Creating and Managing Custom App Roles
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/access_control/manage_custom_app_roles
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/access_control/manage_custom_app_roles

### Creating and Managing Custom App Roles

#### Before you begin

Restriction: You can not change access rights assigned to default roles (Administrator, Co-owner, Viewer) and you can not delete default roles. If you require other rights, create custom roles and assign users to them.
To create, modify, or delete custom roles, you or others must have Manage Access rights. By default, members of the Administrators and Co-owners roles possess these rights. Additionally, you can assign Manage Access rights to a custom role.

#### About this task

App access management enables you to exceed the restrictions of default roles and create roles tailored to your organization. You can manage access to those roles as you would for the default roles.

#### Procedure

- Open the Apps view, and beside the published app whose roles you want to manage, select More > Manage Access .

- In the Console Access tab, select Roles .

- For the following available tasks, follow the corresponding procedure: | | Task | Procedure
| Creating a Custom Role | Default roles may not cover every access requirement. SOTI Snap provides the ability to create roles to suit your needs. Follow the steps below to create a custom role:

- Select the button.

- From the Add Role pop-up, enter a Role Name . Next, select Add .

- In the Custom dropdown, select to list the custom roles.

- Select your new custom role, and select App Permissions .

- In the App Permissions panel, select the checkbox for each permission you want to give the role.

- Select Save .

Results: Your custom role with associated access rights is ready to assign to users.
| Modifying a Custom Role | If the requirements of a custom role have changed, you may update the rights assigned to it by performing the following steps:

- Pick your role from the Custom dropdown list.

- Select App Permissions .

- In the App Permissions panel, select or clear the checkbox for each permission you want to enable or deny the role.

- Select Save .

Results: Your custom role with updated access rights is ready. Note: This only applies to users the next time they log in.
| Deleting a Custom Role | If a custom role is no longer required, you may remove it by performing the following steps:
CAUTION: Deleting a custom role removes the role's access from all role members and cannot be undone. Make sure the remaining access, if any, is appropriate for all users before deleting a role.

- Select to expand the Custom dropdown list and reveal the role names.

- Beside your custom role, select More . Select Delete .

- In the Delete Role? confirmation window, select Delete .

Results: SOTI Snap removes the custom role from the list.

# SOTI Snap help — Managing Role Administration (Device Access) for Business Apps
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/access_control/manage_device_access_for_business_apps_2
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/access_control/manage_device_access_for_business_apps_2

### Managing Role Administration (Device Access) for Business Apps

#### Before you begin

You must configure Device Access in addition to Console Access . For more information about Console Access , see Creating and Managing Users Within Roles .

#### About this task

Business apps require users to sign into their devices when opening the application. The business app logs the user's activities with their validated account to ensure that actions such as approvals belong to the user.

#### Managing Roles

##### About this task

The Device Access tab's Roles sub-panel displays the available roles to which you can assign visibility for private app components. Each role can contain any number (including 0) of device users and device user groups. Each role has a set of SOTI Snap permissions for each of the private app components. By default, there is always an Admin role that SOTI Snap automatically assigns to all new app components. You can not edit or delete this role. s
From the Device Access tab's Roles sub-panel, you can:

- Select New Role to create a new role, see Creating a New Role for details.

- For a created role, you can select to:
- Edit the role's name.

- Duplicate the role and its permissions. (The role's member users and groups are not duplicated.)

- Delete the role. You must confirm the deletion.

- For a role, select the role to access it's Users , Groups or Permissions sections. Important: When you make any changes to a role, it is immediately reflected in the device's SOTI Snap Device Agent.

- Users : Displays all the device users for this role. For any user, select to:
- Assign a role. Select Assign Role to configure the all the roles this user is a member of. This includes the current role.

- Remove a user. Select Remove User to remove the current user from this role.

- Groups : Displays all groups of device users for this role. For any group, select to:
- Assign a role. Select Assign Role to configure all the roles this group is a member of. This includes the current role.

- Remove a group. Select Remove Group to remove the current group from this role.

- Permissions : Displays this role's permissions to all existing app elements. You can customize this role's permission as needed. For more details, see Permissions .

##### Creating a New Role

###### About this task

When you create a new device access role, you must select its members (device users and device user groups) and customize it with relevant permissions. Once created, you can assign these roles to app components.

###### Procedure

- In the Role Details section, enter a name for the role in the Role Name field.

- Select Next .

- In the Permissions section, assign the role's respective permissions for all existing app components ( app elements ).
- For form-based app components (Form, Workflow, AI Form Builder):

- View : Role members can view submitted form records.
- My Records : The role member can only view their own submitted form records.

- Device Records : The role member can view all submitted form records from the device they are using.

- You can also add and then select other filters. See Filtering & Sorting for more details.

- If you enable more than one filter, you can choose the default filter from the Set as Default drop-down menu. The chosen filter is the one that SOTI Snap Device Agent automatically uses for the device user.

- Create : Role members can fill in form details and submit form records.

- Edit : Role members can edit a submitted form record.

- Delete : Role members can delete a submitted form record.

- For non-form-based app components (Page, Webpage):

- View : Role members has visibility of the app element.

- For the Assign Users and Groups section:
- For the Environment Type field, choose the publishing environment that you must publish in order for the role's member to receive the role's permission.
- Production

- Test

- For the Assign Users field, select to add a new device user to this role.

- For the Assign Groups field, select to add a new device group to this role.

###### What to do next

Assign the role to private app components or hamburger menu items with enabled Authentication. See Toggle the Roles That Can Access the App Component for details. Once assigned, the role's members (device users and device user groups) has visibility to them.

#### Managing Users

##### Adding Users to Device Access

###### About this task

To configure users for access to business apps, perform the following:

###### Procedure

- Open the Apps view, and beside the published app whose access you want to update, select More > Manage Access .

- In the Device Access tab, select Role Administration button, then select the Users sub-panel.

- Select the Assign User button. The Assign Users window opens.

- Go to the search field. Enter and select the name of the user(s) you wish to add.

- For the Role(s) dropdown menu, assign at least one role for the name(s) you added.

- Select Add .

###### Results

The added users are now present in the All App Users list.

##### Removing Users from Device Access

###### About this task

To remove user access to business apps, perform the following:

###### Procedure

- Open the Apps view, and beside the published app whose access you want to update, select More > Manage Access .

- In the Device Access tab, select Role Administration button, then the select Users sub-panel. A list of all app users appears in the panel to the right.

- Beside the name of the user you want to remove, select More . Select Remove User .

- In the Remove Selected User(s)? confirmation window, select Remove .

###### Results

From the All App Users list, SOTI Snap removes the user.

#### Managing Groups

##### Adding Groups to Device Access

###### About this task

To configure groups for access to business apps, perform the following:

###### Procedure

- Open the Apps view, and beside the published app whose access you want to update, select More > Manage Access .

- In the Device Access tab, select Role Administration button, then select the Groups sub-panel.

- Select the Assign Group button. The Assign Group window opens.

- Go to the search field. Enter and select the name of the group(s) you wish to add.

- For the Role(s) dropdown menu, assign at least one role for the name(s) you added.

- Select Add .

###### Results

The added groups are now present in the Directory Groups list.

##### Removing Groups from Device Access

###### About this task

To remove group access to business apps, perform the following:

###### Procedure

- Open the Apps view, and beside the published app whose access you want to update, select More > Manage Access .

- In the Device Access tab, select Role Administration button, then select Groups sub-panel. A list of all directory groups appears in the panel to the right.

- Beside the name of the group you want to remove, select More . Select Remove Group .

- In the Remove Selected Group(s)? confirmation window, select Remove .

###### Results

The group is now removed from the Directory Groups .

# SOTI Snap help — Managing Access to Your Apps
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/access_control/set_accesscontrol
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/access_control/set_accesscontrol

### Managing Access to Your Apps

By default, only you (and SOTI Snap administrators) can make changes to your apps. However, you can assign access rights to users using roles.
From Console Access , you can assign users of your SOTI Snap apps to one of three default roles:
- Administrator: Administrators have full rights to manage the app.

- Co-owner: Co-owners have rights to all functions except to delete the app.

- Viewer: Viewers may only duplicate, preview, or examine app versions.

Administrators, Co-owners, and users granted manage access rights can create and assign new custom roles for specific uses.
See the following sections for details on the use, management, and memberships within roles:
- Creating and Managing Custom App Roles

- Creating and Managing Users Within Roles

Business apps require device access in addition to console access. See Managing Role Administration (Device Access) for Business Apps .

# SOTI Snap help — Viewing a Workflow Designer Snapshot
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/access_control/view_a_workflow_snapshot
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/access_control/view_a_workflow_snapshot

### Viewing a Workflow Designer Snapshot

#### About this task

With SOTI Snap , you can review earlier workflow versions and step through changes to your workflow. Seeing prior versions allows you to pinpoint when changes were made or decide if you should revisit them. To view the workflow of any version of your app, do the following:

#### Procedure

- Select the Apps tab. The App Manager opens, showing available apps.

- Beside your app, select More > Version . The Version History of [Your_app_name_here] window opens, showing a list of application versions.

- Next to the desired version, select More > Workflow Designer Snapshot . The Workflow Designer Snapshot window opens.

- Inspect the workflow. Select Close when complete.

Related information
- Building A Business App

# SOTI Snap help — Activity
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/activity
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/activity

### Activity

#### About this task

To review the activity of any request, perform the following steps:

#### Procedure

- Open SOTI Snap on your mobile device.

- In SOTI Snap , open your custom business app and enter your credentials. The business app opens to show two pages: Approvals and Records .

- Go to the page with your request. Tap the request to open it.

- At the top of the form, tap the More button. The following options appear:

- Select Activity . A timeline of workflow activities and outcomes appears.

Related information
- Requesting Management from the App

# SOTI Snap help — Adding a Condition
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/add_a_condition
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/add_a_condition

### Adding a Condition

#### About this task

Use the Condition activity to direct the workflow based on predefined criteria.

#### Procedure

- In the Workflow Canvas , open the Project tab.

- Select the Workflow dropdown. The Workflow Designer opens.

- Open the Activities tab.

- Drag the Condition workflow activity onto the Workflow Designer .

- In the Properties panel, enter a name for the activity in the Display Text field. The name appears on the icon in the workflow.

- Select the Condition(s) button. The Check Condition window opens. Use this window to define the criteria for directing your workflow.

- On the Check Condition panel, choose whether at least one condition or all conditions in your criteria must be met:
- any

- all

- In Field , select the field name on whose value you base the criteria.

- In Operator , select the method to evaluate the field value:
- Equal to

- Not equal to

- Contains

- Starts with

- Greater than

- Less than

- Is empty

- Is not empty

- In the Value field, enter the value you are comparing against the field's value. Tip: The Value field does not appear for the choices Is empty and Is not empty .

- To add another condition, select Add .

- Select Save in the SOTI Snap banner to apply your changes.

Related information
- Available Activities

- Building A Business App

- Using Workflow Variables

# SOTI Snap help — Add a Data Export
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/add_a_data_export
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/add_a_data_export

### Add a Data Export

#### About this task

Use the Data Export activity in workflows requiring the export of app data.

#### Procedure

- In the Workflow Canvas , open the Project tab.

- Select the Workflow dropdown. The Workflow Designer opens.

- Open the Activities tab.

- Drag the Data Export workflow activity onto the Workflow Designer .

- In the Properties panel, enter a name for the activity in the Display Text field. The name appears on the icon in the workflow.

- In the Service menu, select the appropriate form from the rules created in the Data Connection Rules page. Tip: Select Add next to the dropdown to create a Data Connection rule. For details, see Creating a Data Export rule .

Related information
- Available Activities

- Using Data Connection Rules

# SOTI Snap help — Add a Group Approval
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/add_a_group_approver
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/add_a_group_approver

### Add a Group Approval

#### About this task

Use the Group Approval activity where the workflow requires approvals from users of a SOTI Snap user group.

#### Procedure

- In the Workflow Canvas , open the Project tab.

- Select the Workflow dropdown. The Workflow Designer opens.

- Open the Activities tab.

- Drag the Group Approval workflow activity onto the Workflow Designer .

- In the Properties panel, enter a name for the activity in the Display Text field. The name appears on the icon in the workflow.

- In the App Approvers field, type the name of a group you would like to request approvals at this part of the workflow. When found, select the group name to add it to your list. You can also choose a Group List Widget you have created and use the widget's group. You can continue to add groups as required. Note: The list of available groups flows from SOTI Identity and includes any integrated third-party identity providers (IDPs).

- Select other Group Approval settings as required. | Number of Approvers | Select how many approvers SOTI Snap requires to approve an activity. Options are:
- All Approvers From Each Group Must Approve

- Specified Number Of Approvers From A Group Must Approve Note: You must enter the number of approvers required.
| Advanced Permissions | Select how SOTI Snap behaves if one of the following scenarios occurs for the added group(s) chosen for the App Approvers field.
- If SOTI Snap detects that all the added group(s) does not have any users.

- If SOTI Snap can not detect all the added group(s).

You can choose from the following behaviour:
- Assign to Another Group : SOTI Snap replaces the original assigned group(s) with a backup group you choose from the Search for groups drop-down menu. After you choose a group, the Number of Approvers field appears again for the chosen backup group.

- Auto Approval : SOTI Snap automatically approves the approval request. The workflow proceeds to the next Activity set by a Approved Group Approval .

- Auto Rejection : SOTI Snap automatically rejects the approval request. The workflow proceeds to the next Activity set by Rejected Group Approval .
| Acknowledgement Settings | Select the following options if you wish to offer approvers with the option to add notes about their decision:
- Enable Approval Notes

- Enable Rejection Notes

- Enable Signature on Approval

- Enable Signature on Rejection

Specify whether notes are Required or Optional for each enabled setting.
| Send Back to Submitter | Select Allow 'Send Back To Submitter' Action if you want the approver to be able to revert the workflow to the submitter for correction and resubmission. If selected, you can also select Enable Notes to offer approvers with the option to add notes about their decision. If enabled, specify whether notes are Required or Optional .
| Edit Settings | Select Allow Editing for Approver(s) if you wish to enable the approver to edit the request.

- Select Save in the SOTI Snap banner to apply your changes.

Related information
- Example: Creating a Business App

# SOTI Snap help — Binding SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/add_a_new_application_in_soti_identity
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/add_a_new_application_in_soti_identity

### Binding SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl

#### Before you begin

- You must Generate SOTI Identity Credentials for SOTI MobiControl to bind SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl .

#### About this task

Use this procedure to bind SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl . This creates a synchronized binding between SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl to enable user authentication with SOTI Identity and log in to SOTI Snap for Lockdown apps with SOTI Identity .

#### Procedure

- Log into the SOTI MobiControl web console as an administrator.

- From the main menu, select Global Settings > SOTI ONE > SOTI Identity . The SOTI Identity configuration panel displays.

- In the SOTI Identity configuration panel, toggle Enable SOTI Identity , and enter the SOTI Identity URL, Client ID, and Client Secret keys obtained in step 9 above.

- Select Save .

- From the main SOTI MobiControl menu, select Global Settings > Console Settings > Authentication Options . The Authentication Options panel displays.

- In the Authentication Options panel, select SOTI Identity for the Authentication Type .

- Select Save .

#### Results

The binding for SOTI MobiControl to SOTI Identity has been completed.

#### What to do next

Proceed to Assigning SOTI MobiControl Users in SOTI Identity to assign users to SOTI MobiControl for user authentication.

Related information
- SOTI ONE Integration with SOTI Snap

- Integrating SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl for Lockdown

- Generate SOTI Identity Credentials for SOTI MobiControl

# SOTI Snap help — Add a Notification
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/add_a_notification
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/add_a_notification

### Add a Notification

#### About this task

Use the Notification activity to send emails when triggered by the workflow.

#### Procedure

- In the Workflow Canvas , open the Project tab.

- Select the Workflow dropdown. The Workflow Designer opens.

- Open the Activities tab.

- Drag and drop the Notification workflow activity onto the Workflow Designer .

- In the Properties panel, enter a name for the activity in the Display Text field. The name appears on the icon in the workflow.

- Click Configure Email Notification . The Compose Template Settings window opens.

- Complete the following fields:
- In the To field, enter the recipients' email addresses. Notifications are typically used to update people external to the workflow.

- In the Subject field, enter the text to appear on the subject line of the email.

- In the Message field, enter the specific information you wish to tell the recipient.

Note: Click the macro icon for any of the above fields to insert values from the request or default values such as the recipient's email address.

- Click Save .

- Select Save in the SOTI Snap banner to apply your changes.

Related information
- Example: Creating a Business App

# SOTI Snap help — Adding a Set Status
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/add_a_set_status
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/add_a_set_status

### Adding a Set Status

#### About this task

Use the Set Status activity to indicate where you are in the workflow.

#### Procedure

- In the Workflow Canvas , open the Project tab.

- Select the Workflow dropdown. The Workflow Designer opens.

- Open the Activities tab.

- Drag the Set Status workflow activity onto the Workflow Designer .

- In the Properties panel, enter a name for the activity in the Display Text field. The name appears on the icon in the workflow.

- Optional: Select the settings icon to reveal the default display values that populate the Set Status dropdown. You can update each value to match your process or accept the defaults. When changes are complete, select Save .

- In the Set Status dropdown, select the process status from your current place in the workflow.

- Select Save in the SOTI Snap banner to apply your changes.

Related information
- Configuring Status Settings

- Building A Business App

# SOTI Snap help — Adding a User Approval
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/add_a_single_approver
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/add_a_single_approver

### Adding a User Approval

#### About this task

Use the User Approval activity where the workflow requires approvals from named users.

#### Procedure

- In the Workflow Canvas , open the Project tab.

- Select the Workflow dropdown. The Workflow Designer opens.

- Open the Activities tab.

- Drag the User Approval workflow activity onto the Workflow Designer .

- In the Properties panel, enter a name for the activity in the Display Text field. The name appears on the icon in the workflow.

- In the Add Approvers field, complete at least one of the following options: Note: The list of available users is from SOTI Identity and includes any integrated third-party identity providers (IdPs).

- Optional: Type the name of a user for whom you would like to grant approvals at this part of the workflow. When found, select the user name to add it to your list. You can continue to add more users as required.

- Optional: Select the Gear icon, then the User List icon to choose a configured User List widget as the source of approver names for the User Approval activity. See Connecting a User List Widget to a Workflow Activity .

- Optional: Select the Gear icon, then the Submitter Manager icon to choose the submitter's manager automatically. See Configuring Submitter Managers .

- Optional: Select the Gear icon, then the Dynamic User Manager icon to choose from a dynamic list of user managers automatically. See Configuring Dynamic User Managers .

- Select other User Approval settings as required. | Number of Approvers | Select how many approvers must approve an activity. Options are:
- All Approvers Must Approve

- A Fixed Number of Approvers Must Approve Note: You must enter the number of approvers required.
| Notes Settings | Select the following options if you wish to offer approvers with the option to add notes about their decision:
- Enable Approval Notes

- Enable Rejection Notes

Specify whether notes are Required or Optional for each enabled setting.
| Send Back to Submitter | Select Allow 'Send Back To Submitter' Action if you want the approver to be able to revert the workflow to the submitter for correction and resubmission. If selected, you can also toggle Enable Notes to allow approvers to add notes about their decisions. If enabled, specify whether notes are Required or Optional .
| Edit Settings | Select Allow Editing for Approver(s) if you wish to allow the approver to change the request.

- Select Save in the SOTI Snap banner to apply your changes.

#### Results

A new user approval process becomes part of the workflow.

Related information
- Connecting a User List Widget to a Workflow Activity

- Submitter Manager

- Dynamic User Manager

- Available Activities

# SOTI Snap help — Adding a REST API
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/adding_a_rest_api
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/adding_a_rest_api

### Adding a REST API

#### About this task

Use the REST API activity in workflows that require REST APIs.

#### Procedure

- In the Workflow Canvas , open the Project tab.

- Select the Workflow dropdown. The Workflow Designer opens.

- Open the Activities tab.

- Drag the REST API workflow activity onto the Workflow Designer .

- In the Properties panel, enter a name for the activity in the Display Text field. The name appears on the icon in the workflow.

- In the Service menu, select the appropriate REST API from the rules created in the Data Connection Rule > REST API tab. Tip: Select next to the dropdown to create a Rest API rule. For details, see REST API: Creating a REST API Rule .

- Select Map Fields to open the Set Fields Mapping dialog box. Then, select Start Mapping .

- Optional: Select Add to create a new workflow variable. From the Add Workflow Variable dialog box, enter the following:
- Variable Name : Enter a name for the workflow variable.

- Description : Enter a description for the workflow variable.

- Value : Enter the value for the workflow variable.

- Map SOTI Snap App Fields to the selected REST API's request parameters. Once you have finished mapping, select Next . Then, select Next again. Restriction: When mapping, each request parameter can receive up to a single app field. However, you can assign app field to as many request parameters as you like.

Remember: When you map a workflow variable to a request parameter, SOTI Snap copies the workflow variable's value and pastes it into the request parameter value when SOTI Snap makes the REST API query.

- Map the selected REST API's response parameters to SOTI Snap Workflow Variables . Once you have finished mapping, select Finish . Restriction: When mapping, each workflow variable can receive up to a single response parameter. However, you can assign each response parameter to as many workflow variables as you like.

Remember: When you map a response parameter to a workflow variable, SOTI Snap copies the response parameter value and pastes it into the workflow variable value when SOTI Snap makes the REST API query.

Related information
- Using Workflow Variables

- Using Data Connection Rules

# SOTI Snap help — Adding a Script
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/adding_a_script
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/adding_a_script

### Adding a Script

#### About this task

Use the Script activity when you want to run a JavaScript in a workflow.

#### Procedure

- In the Workflow Canvas , open the Project tab.

- Select the Workflow dropdown. The Workflow Designer opens.

- Open the Activities tab.

- Drag the Script workflow activity onto the Workflow Designer .

- In the Properties panel, enter a name for the activity in the Display Text field. The name appears on the icon in the workflow.

- In the Script menu, select Run JavaScript . Then, select Script Editor so you can enter JavaScript code. See Run JavaScript for more details. Tip: You can get and set the value of workflow variables using the following JavaScript functions:
- Snap.wfVariables.getValue

- Snap.wfVariables.setValue

Related information
- Using Workflow Variables

- Run JavaScript

# SOTI Snap help — Adding a Set Value
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/adding_a_set_value
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/adding_a_set_value

### Adding a Set Value

#### About this task

Use the Set Value activity to add and update workflow variables and map them to SOTI Snap form fields. Important: This is the only Activity where you can change form fields using workflow variables.

#### Procedure

- In the Workflow Canvas , open the Project tab.

- Select the Workflow dropdown. The Workflow Designer opens.

- Open the Activities tab.

- Drag the Set Value workflow activity onto the Workflow Designer .

- In the Properties panel, enter a name for the activity in the Display Text field. The name appears on the icon in the workflow.

- In the Set Value menu, select Update Field Mapping .

- From the Set Fields Mapping dialog that displays, select Next .

- Optional: Select Add to create a new workflow variable. From the Add Workflow Variable dialog box, enter the following:
- Variable Name : Enter a name for the workflow variable.

- Description : Enter a description for the workflow variable.

- Value : Enter the value for the workflow variable.

- Map SOTI Snap Workflow Variables to SOTI Snap Form Fields . Once you have finished mapping, select Finish . Restriction: When mapping, each form field can receive up to a single workflow variable. However, you can assign each workflow variable to as many form fields as you like.

Remember: Once you map a workflow variable to a form field, any automatic change to the workflow variable's value is automatically applied to the form field. The workflow variable can automatically change by Script or Rest API . However, any change to the form field (such as by the device user's input) does not change the workflow variable.

Related information
- Using Workflow Variables

# SOTI Snap help — Adding App Components
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/adding_app_components
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/adding_app_components

### Adding App Components

#### About this task

The types of screens you use to build your app define its structure. Your app can host many screen styles, and the number of screens you can add is not limited.

#### Procedure

- In the Canvas , select the Project tab.

- Select Add to open the Add App Components screens dialog box.

- Select the app component type you like to use.
- Form: Use this screen type to collect data from app users through your app. For example, surveys. You can add many forms to an app, each corresponding to a different task, such as managing orders, adding items to inventory, or editing product information. You can add many forms (or pages) to your app to distinguish between sections. Note: All apps automatically have at least one form or page screen.

- Page: Use this screen type if the app does not require data submissions from the user. Pages are useful for displaying information and triggering actions, such as embedding maps or disseminating training content. You can add more pages (or forms) to your app to distinguish between sections. Note: All apps automatically have at least one form or page screen.

- WebPage: Use this type to embed external web content directly within your app. App users can browse that website without leaving your SOTI Snap app.

- Hamburger: Use this type to add a navigation element to your app. The hamburger type simplifies navigation by adding a menu that directs the user to different locations in the app. Restriction: You can only add one Hamburger Menu per app.

- AI Form Builder: Use this type to automatically create a form from a prompt message or a file ( *.pdf , *.jpeg , *.jpg , *.png , *.txt , or *.doc ). For the prompt message, you can describe the details of the form you envision, and the AI generates as such for you. Otherwise, you can upload a file containing the form you want to generate, and SOTI Snap creates the necessary input fields and options. Restriction: The maximum file size is 10MB.

- For the Name field, enter a brief name.

- For the selected app component, do the following respective procedure: | | Type | Procedure
| Form | How To: Build a Form-Based App in SOTI Snap
To add a form screen to your app:

- For Gridview / Page Layout , choose one of the available layouts. You can rotate the layout you choose. See Gridview Layout for more details.

- Select Add .

- Select the new form you created in the app screen list and not the new form's sub page. Notice: When you add a new form, a subpage is automatically created for the new form as well. SOTI Snap by default selects the sub page and not the new form.

- In the Form Properties panel on the right, configure the form.

- Optional: Nest more pages or web pages under the form.
| Page | To add a page to your app, do the following:

- For Gridview / Page Layout , choose one of the available layouts. You can rotate the layout you choose. See Gridview Layout for more details.

- Select Add .

- In the Page Properties in the right panel, perform the following tasks:
- For the ID field: SOTI Snap automatically generates and uses a unique identifier for a new page. You can change this field's value. If you do change it, set it to the same as the Name field.

- For the Name field: This is the name you entered earlier when creating the page. You use this field when referring to this page (such as when incorporating Events and Action ) or organizing pages. If you have not done so already, enter a unique customized name.

- For the Page Title drop-down list, select one of the following labels:
- Same as page name

- Same as form name

- Same as app name

- Custom name

- Design the background of the page. Do one of the following:
- Color : Select Color Scale to choose a solid color to fill the background.

- Image : Select Upload Image to upload an image to use it as a background for this page. Then select the Scale drop-down menu to choose a setting.
- Fit

- Fill

- Stretch

- Tile

- Center

- Switch to the Format tab to edit the padding and margin of the page.

- Switch to the Events tab to configure the events.
| WebPage | To add a web page to your app, do the following:

- Select Add .

- In the Webpage Properties in the right panel, choose how a Header name label option from the drop-down list. A header name appears at the top of the app screen while on a specific screen.
- Same as page name

- Same as app name

- Custom name

- Do one of the following to specify a URL: Important: The specified URL must began with HTTP or HTTPS .

- Enter the URL of the web page you want to embed in your app.

- Select a Global Variable that has a value set to a URL or select an App Field (widget) that has text set to a URL.

You can specify the direct URL to the page you want to initially display. You can also specify a base URL of the entire site and then use parameters to point to the specific page that you want to display. Parameters are useful when the link is likely to differ slightly between users, such as the addition of an authentication or other security features.
Optional: For the URL, define the keys and assign their corresponding values to the properties below.

- Headers: Use headers when you want to grant app users access to secured webpages. Only visitors with the header values can access the web page. Restriction: Only the website developer can supply the headers defined for a page.

- Query Strings: Use query strings to display the results of a search.

- Cookies: Use cookies to keep users logged into a web page by passing token or session keys and values. SOTI Snap use cookies to enable automatic authentication to a logged in user so they do not need to log in each time they access the website.

- Path Parameters: Use path parameters to generate more dynamic links.
| Hamburger Menu | Restriction: You can only have one hamburger menu enabled per app.

Tip: Consider adding a hamburger menu at the end of your app building process so that you can configure all the menu items at once rather than as you create them.

To add a hamburger menu to your app:

- Select Add .

- In the Menu Properties panel on the right, make sure that SOTI Snap has toggled Show Hamburger Menu switch to on.

- Select the Menu Item tab, and then select the Add button to add a new menu item.

- Enter a new name for the menu item and select the image icon to upload a image. SOTI Snap associates the menu item with the image you have uploaded in the hamburger menu on the app. Repeat for as many menu items as you want to appear in the Hamburger menu.

- Optional: Switch to the Header tab and toggle the option to Enabled .
- Select the image icon under Display photo to upload an image that appears in the Hamburger Menu.

- Enter a name in the Display name field.

- Switch to the Format tab to edit the appearance of the hamburger menu.

- Switch to the Events tab.

- From the Hamburger drop-down list, select a menu or header item.

- Beside Actions , select Add and select and define an action from the Actions drop-down list.

- Repeat for the rest of the menu and header items and select Save .
| Al Form Builder | Digitizing Processes with the SOTI Snap AI Form Builder
To add an AI form builder to your app:

- Do one of the following:
- Select Prompt to enter a prompt message to generate your form:
- Optional: Select Lead Generation Form or Feedback Form to automatically fill a prepared prompt message.

- Below Enter the prompt and see SOTI Snap AI in action , enter a prompt message in the text box describing the form you want to generate. Tip: The built-in AI has the following features:
- Support for 100+ languages.

- Generate sophisticated contextual images upon request.

- Embed relevant videos upon request.

- For each of the requested widgets, their properties are automatically filled out with appropriate values.

- Select PDF/Image to upload a file to generate your form: Note: The maximum file size is 10MB.
Choose from one of the following:
- Optional : Select Learn more to see more details about this feature.

- Optional : Select Feedback to submit your name, email and a message to SOTI about this feature.

- From your local device's file explorer, drag a *.pdf , *.jpeg , *.jpg , *.png , *.txt , or *.doc file into the Drag and drop image file here file selector box. A file icon or preview appears.

-
- Select to open a file browser.

- From the file browser, navigate to the *.pdf , *.jpeg , *.jpg , *.png , *.txt , or *.doc file containing your form.

- Select the file. A file icon or preview appears.

- Select Generate With AI . Note: It may take a few minutes for SOTI Snap to automatically generate the form.

Result: You have generated a new form, which opens in the canvas for editing. You can now edit and customize your new form.

- The edits are temporary and unsaved. From the top-right, select Save to confirm the adding of the new app component. The new app component is now permanently saved.

Related information
- Building A Standard App

# SOTI Snap help — Adding a SOTI Snap Logout Action to a Widget
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/adding_snap_logout_actions_to_widgets
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/adding_snap_logout_actions_to_widgets

### Adding a SOTI Snap Logout Action to a Widget

#### Before you begin

- Sign in as a SOTI Snap user with the Apps permission. See Users and Permissions for more information.

- Create a Lockdown app that contains a widget that supports events. See Building a Lockdown App for more information.

- Include one or more SOTI Snap business apps with a configured sign-in workflow in the Lockdown app. See Building A Business App for more information.

#### About this task

Use this procedure to add the Snap Logout action to a widget event in a Lockdown app. When the associated event occurs, such as selecting a button, SOTI Snap signs the user out of the SOTI Snap Device Agent session.

#### Procedure

- In the SOTI Snap main menu, open Apps .

- Locate the app to configure and select it to open SOTI Snap Lockdown Designer .

- In the SOTI Snap Lockdown Designer , add or select a widget that supports configuring events. Note: Not all widgets support configuring events. See Available Widgets for the list of supported widgets.

- Select Events in the upper-right corner.

- In the Actions section, select to add an action.

- Select Snap Logout .

- Configure the options in the Snap Logout window.

- Optional: Enable Log Out Without Prompt to bypass the sign-out confirmation window.

- Optional: Enter a message in Snap Log Out Successful Message . SOTI Snap Device Agent displays this message on the device after the user completes the sign-out confirmation prompt.

- Select Add .

#### Results

The Snap Logout action is added to the widget event. When the event occurs on a device, SOTI Snap Device Agent starts the sign-out process.

#### What to do next

Publish the Lockdown app and deploy the updated version to devices to make the action available to users. See Devices: Publishing and Distributing Apps for more information.

Related information
- Building a Lockdown App

# SOTI Snap help — Assemblies: Adding a Collection Assembly
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/assemblies/assemblies
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/assemblies/assemblies

### Assemblies: Adding a Collection Assembly

#### About this task

Assemblies are sets of widgets (see Widgets ) that you can configure to work together to deliver advanced app functionality. Assemblies speed up app development by grouping related widgets together and automating commonly required app functionality.
A collection assembly simplifies app record management by allowing app users to manage records from within SOTI Snap .
The following video illustrates how to add a collection assembly to your app:

#### Procedure

- Open your app in the Canvas .

- Select the Assemblies tab.

- Select Collection to open the Create Collection dialog box.

- Select the actions you want to enable in the app to manage records. Each action button has display text, which you can change to suit your app.

- Select Finish .

#### Results

Two new screens appear in the App Settings panel. The first screen displays app records while the second screen has the widgets that collect data from your app users.

#### What to do next

Add the widgets that collect data from your app users.

Related information
- Using Widgets

- Available Widgets

# SOTI Snap help — Assigning SOTI MobiControl Users in SOTI Identity
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/assign_users_identity
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/assign_users_identity

### Assigning SOTI MobiControl Users in SOTI Identity

#### Before you begin

- You must Bind SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl before assigning users in SOTI Identity .

#### About this task

Use this procedure to assign SOTI MobiControl users in SOTI Identity to enable user authentication and role assignment with SOTI MobiControl . This enables SOTI MobiControl to authenticate users through SOTI Identity and other third-party identity providers such as Microsoft Azure and OKTA.

#### Procedure

- Log into the SOTI Identity web console as an administrator.

- From the main menu, select Applications .
The Applications dashboard displays.

- Select the SOTI MobiControl application, then select Assign Users .

- In the Assign Users/Groups panel, select the Add icon and to add a user with administrator permissions. Note: The roles are fetched from SOTI MobiControl .

- Select Assign to assign the role to the user.

- Select Done to confirm the assignment.

#### Results

Users are now assigned to SOTI MobiControl from SOTI Identity based on their selected role.

#### What to do next

Confirm if users can log in to SOTI Identity and access the SOTI MobiControl application. Proceed with Integrating SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap for Lockdown if SOTI MobiControl has not been bound to a SOTI Snap instance or proceed to Creating a New App or Publishing Apps in SOTI Snap .

Related information
- SOTI ONE Integration with SOTI Snap

- Integrating SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl for Lockdown

- Generate SOTI Identity Credentials for SOTI MobiControl

- Binding SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl

# SOTI Snap help — Available Activities
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/available_activities
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/available_activities

### Available Activities

The Workflow Designer supplies a selection of the following activities. | | Widget Name | Description
| Condition | Directs workflow based on predefined criteria.
| Data Export | Exports data through data connection rules as part of the workflow.
| Group Approval | Prevents workflow from advancing until members of a SOTI Snap user group approve.
| Notification | Sends notification emails to external users (outside of the workflow) to inform them of workflow status.
| Rest API | Performs CRUD operations on web resources by using REST API rules.
| Script | Adds JavaScript codes in Workflow Designer.
| Set Status | Sets the current status of activity within the workflow.
| Set Value | Updates records with script/REST API responses.
| User Approval | Prevents the workflow from advancing until named users approve.

Related information
- Workflow Designer

- Add and Delete Workflow Activities

- Using Workflow Variables

# SOTI Snap help — Building A Standard App
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/build_apps
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/build_apps

### Building A Standard App

Begin building a Standard app within SOTI Snap .

#### Before you begin

- You must start creating the app through the App Manager in SOTI Snap . See Creating a New App for more information.

#### About this task

Use this procedure to begin creating new SOTI Snap standard applications.

#### Procedure

- From the SOTI Snap menu, select Apps and select New Standard App .

- When building a Standard App , you can start completely from scratch with a Blank App or use the Form Based App template to collect data from app users. Important:
- You can not transform a Blank App to a Form App later.

- Blank App 's can not send/receive data.

- From the Create Standard App or dialog box, you can make the following customizations: Tip: You can change these customizations later.

- Hover over the app icon and select Edit to choose a new image to use as the app Icon .

- Enter the Name of the app.

- Enter a Description of the app. Restriction: You may enter only up to 100 characters.

- Choose a Theme color for your app. SOTI Snap chooses #044d7c by default.

- Expand the dropdown list to choose a Font for your app. SOTI Snap chooses Roboto-Regular by default. See Uploading Custom Fonts to add a custom font.

- Change the app's background image by selecting Upload image . You can otherwise change the background using Select background to access an existing image gallery of App Background s. From here, you can choose a template image from gallery and then Select Background .

- Choose one of the available Gridview/App Layouts . You can rotate the layout you choose. See Gridview Layout for more details.

- Add screens and navigation to your SOTI Snap apps to provides different app screen options to accommodate different app usage. For example, you can add a Hamburger Menu option to assist in easier navigation within the app or embed external webpages directly within the app.

- Add Widgets to the SOTI Snap app to add additional form and function. Drag and rearrange widgets within the canvas based on user requirement. Learn more at Using Widgets .

- Add Data Connections to your Widgets to exchange information between your SOTI Snap app and external systems. Use Data Connections to pull data into your app or send data from your app to a third-party tool. Learn more at Using Data Connection Rules .

- Preview your app by selecting (Preview) to generate a preview of the application to see how it looks on a device before deployment. Learn more at Managing Built Apps .

- Prepare for publication by creating enrollment rules (to control who can download your apps) and groups (to control who gets which apps). Important: This step may be skipped if you are deploying SOTI Snap apps within SOTI MobiControl , See Enrolling Devices into SOTI MobiControl and Installing SOTI Snap with Apps for more information.

- Publish your app to enable users to download and interact with published apps through the SOTI Snap Device Agent, a container for all your custom apps. Learn more at Publishing an App .

#### Results

An SOTI Snap Standard app has been built to meet user and administrator requirements. Any apps you create appears in the Apps Manager under My Apps , even if you have not have not published them yet.

#### What to do next

Once the app is ready for deployment, proceed to publish the apps to devices or publish to SOTI MobiControl through App Policies. See Devices: Publishing and Distributing Apps for instructions.

Related information
- Adding App Components

# SOTI Snap help — Building A Business App
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/build_business_apps
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/build_business_apps

### Building A Business App

#### About this task

Business apps add an extra dimension to your SOTI Snap apps by working with your business processes to automate tasks, grant actions, reduce manual errors, and produce an auditable trail of activities. Feature Focus: Introducing Business Apps
SOTI Snap Feature Focus Workflow Feature Use-Case Demonstration
It is simple and easy to build business apps in SOTI Snap . To get started, follow the steps below.

#### Procedure

- Log into the SOTI Snap web console.

- From the main menu, select Apps . The App Manager panel displays.

- In the App Manager , select New App . A pop-up lists new app options.

- Select Create Business App . The Create Business App panel opens.

- Complete the form information fields. | Icon | Select the Edit button to select an image to use as an icon for your app.
| Name | Give a name for your app. This name displays in the list of applications in the App Manager .
| Description | Describe the purpose or function of your app.
| Theme | Select a theme color for your app.
| Font | Select the font you wish to apply to the canvas and the app. See Using Custom Fonts for more details.
| App background | Upload an image to set a background for your app.

- Select Save and Continue .

#### Results

With the basic structure of your app created, develop the forms and workflow to support it. See Building A Standard App and Workflow Designer to progress your app further. Any apps you create appear in the App Manager under My Apps , even if you have not published them. Important: Remember to secure your apps. See Managing Access to Your Apps . Business apps also require you to authorize specific devices to use the app. This ensures the device’s owner is associated with any workflow approvals. See Managing Role Administration (Device Access) for Business Apps .

#### Example

For a complete example of the creation of a workflow, see Example: Creating a Business App .

Related information
- Workflow Designer

- Building A Standard App

# SOTI Snap help — Building a Lockdown App
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/building_a_lockdown_app
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/building_a_lockdown_app

### Building a Lockdown App

Lockdown mode restricts device functionality so users can only access approved apps and features. It creates a custom home screen that displays only authorized applications. Use lockdown apps to prevent tampering, limit distractions, and enforce compliance in enterprise environments.

#### Before you begin

- Your environment must be a Managed SOTI Snap instance and integrated with SOTI Identity . For more information, see: System Requirements .

- Log in as a SOTI Snap user with the Lockdown Designer role assigned in SOTI Identity . See SOTI Snap Lockdown Designer Licensing .

#### About this task

The SOTI Snap Lockdown app wizard empowers organizations to design tailored kiosk-style applications that limit user access to approved apps and features on a device. With an intuitive wizard, administrators can customize themes, layouts, and visibility settings while assigning apps and actions suited to specific operational needs. This solution guarantees a secure, focused experience, making it perfect for shared or enterprise-managed devices. By developing a lockdown app using the SOTI Snap Lockdown wizard, organizations no longer need to manually create lockdown screens using HTML and CSS. This streamlined solution simplifies the process, allowing for efficient customization without coding. Note: The SOTI Snap Lockdown wizard is currently available only for Android Work Managed devices.

#### Procedure

To launch the SOTI Snap Lockdown wizard and create a lockdown app:

- Log into the SOTI Snap web console as administrator.

- From the main menu, select the Lockdown tab.

Creating a new lockdown app

- Select the ( ) New lockdown app icon.
The Create lockdown app screen displays.

- In the Set up your lockdown app panel, enter an App name and choose a predefined theme.

Creating your own theme:

- Optional: Instead of choosing a predefined theme, you can create your own theme by selecting the new Themes ( ) icon (green box in the screenshot above).
The Add new theme panel displays. Enter a Theme name and configure the theme in the Settings panel.

Select Save .

- Select Next . The Set up unauthenticated lockdown Home screen panel displays.

Configuring the unauthenticated lockdown home screen:

- Optional: To configure an unauthenticated lockdown home screen, enable Set up unauthenticated lockdown Home screen . Unauthenticated lockdowns do not require a user login or specific conditions.

- Enter a Home screen name and select a template.
Note: The selected template is used as the default for all unauthenticated home screens in the app. You can customize unauthenticated home screens later.

- Select Next .

Configuring the pre-authentication home screen:

- To configure a pre-authentication lockdown home screen, enable Set up pre-authentication screen . Pre-authentication home screens appear before a device is logged in to. Use them to offer limited access to public content or features that do not require user authentication.

- Enter a Home screen name and select a template.
Note: The selected template is used as the default for all pre-unauthenticated home screens in the app. You can customize pre-authenticated home screens later.

- Select Next .

Configuring the post-authentication home screen:

- To configure a post-authentication lockdown home screen, enable Set up post-authentication screen . Post-authentication home screens appear after a device is logged in to. Use them to provide users with access suitable for their role.

- Enter a Home screen name and select a template.
Note: The selected template is used as the default for all post-unauthenticated home screens in the app. You can customize post-authenticated home screens later.

- Select Next .

Configuring the speed lockdown home screen:

- To configure a speed lockdown home screen, enable Set up speed lockdown screen . Speed lockdown is enforced if the device travels above a set limit. Use this to provide users with a vehicle-friendly interface or limit device usage on the road.

- Enter a Home screen name and select a template.
Note: The selected template is used as the default for all speed lockdown home screens in the app. You can customize speed lockdown home screens later.

- Select Next .

Configuring the cellular lockdown home screen:

- To configure a cellular lockdown home screen, enable Set up cellular lockdown screen . Cellular lockdown is enforced if a SIM card from an unauthorized cellular carrier is detected. Use this to secure your device.

- Enter a Home screen name and select a template.
Note: The selected template is used as the default for all cellular lockdown home screens in the app. You can customize cellular lockdown home screens later.

- Select Next .

Configuring the out of contact home screen:

- To configure an out of contact home screen, enable Set up out of contact screen . An out of contact home screen is shown when the device disconnects from the SOTI MobiControl server. Use this to secure devices you are unable to communicate with.

- Enter a Home screen name and select a template.
Note: The selected template is used as the default for all out of contact home screens in the app. You can customize out of contact home screens later.

- Select Next .

Configuring the Geofence lockdown home screen:

- To configure a geofence lockdown home screen, enable Set up geofence screen . Geofence lockdown is enforced if the device leaves a defined perimeter. Use this to secure devices.

- Enter a Home screen name and select a template.
Note: The selected template is used as the default for all Geofence lockdown home screens in the app. You can customize Geofence lockdown home screens later.

- Select Next .

Configuring the low battery lockdown home screen:

- To configure a low battery home screen, enable Set up low battery screen . The low battery home screen is shown if the device's battery level falls below a set percentage. Use this to notify users to take steps to minimize disruptions due to uncharged devices.

- Enter a Home screen name and select a template.
Note: The selected template is used as the default for all low battery home screens in the app. You can customize low battery home screens later.

- Select Finish .

#### What to do next

Use the lockdown configuration you created using the SOTI Snap Lockdown app wizard in SOTI MobiControl .

Related information
- Building A Standard App

- Building A Business App

# SOTI Snap help — Approval Management
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/con_approval_management
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/con_approval_management

### Approval Management

Manage approval requests from any internet-connected device's web browser. Use the SOTI Snap Approval Management tab to manage approvals by viewing, tracking, assigning, and setting approval request statuses.
Approval Management functions are in the following tabs:

- All —From this tab, administrators can see all active requests and reassign them to other approvers. See View and Reassign Approvals .

- Approvals —In this tab, individual approvers can see and interact with all requests that involve them. Approvals are a web console alternative to managing approvals in an app . See Request Management from the Web Console .

Related information
- Requesting Management from the App

# SOTI Snap help — Gridview Layout
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/con_gridview_layout
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/con_gridview_layout

### Gridview Layout

You can improve navigation and user experience by selecting a Gridview layout for new pages and forms.
A Gridview layout divides the display space into up to three adjustable sections. Use a button or image widget's On Click event to control the content in each section.

The Gridview layout option delivers the following user benefits:
- Ease of use —Applications feel more native as users explore the application seamlessly without leaving their starting point.

- Enhanced navigation —Content loads dynamically based on user selections while the original navigation options remain.

- Reduced clutter —Dividing the display space into sections better organizes the presentation of information. Users can focus on specific areas of interest without the distraction of excessive content or cluttered layouts.

For further layout customization, you can also incorporate Layout widgets. Unlike Gridview layouts, Layout widgets only offer customization options where the screen splits from left to right, not up to down.

Related information
- Selecting a Gridview Layout

- Example: Setting up and Using a Gridview Layout

- Add a Layout Widget
