# SOTI Snap help — Configuring Approvals
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/configure_approvals
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/configure_approvals

### Configuring Approvals

As much as you design your workflow to suit your organization's processes, you should also develop Approvals to match your company's terms and references. SOTI Snap lets you control what the user sees when interacting with the Approvals List Properties and the Approvals Detail Properties . The following sections list the properties you can configure. For information about how to use Approvals , see Managing Approvals .

#### Approvals List Properties

The Approvals List Properties displays requests relevant to the approver, including pending, approved, rejected, or sent-back requests. The following video shows how to modify the Approvals List Properties visual elements to match your workflow.

The following tables list all the configurables for the Approvals List Properties . Review updates on the Canvas as you make changes.
| | Property | Description
| ID | Assign a unique identifier value to the widget used to refer to it in the script.
| Name | Accept the default or set a custom name for the Approvals List Properties page.
| Page Title | Set the page title for viewing the request list. Options are:
- Same as form name

- Same as app name

- Custom name (specified in the Display Text property)
| Display Text | This property is visible when you set Page Title to Custom name . Set a custom page title for viewing the request list.

#### Approvals Detail Properties

The Approvals Detail Properties interacts with details of the request. The following video shows how you can modify the visible elements of Approvals Detail Properties to match your workflow.

The following tables list the configurables for Approvals Detail Properties . Review updates on the Canvas as you make changes.

Related information
- Managing Approvals

- Building A Business App

# SOTI Snap help — Configuring Records
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/configure_records
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/configure_records

### Configuring Records

The Records interface for mobile devices is highly configurable to suit your organization's processes, terms, and references. SOTI Snap enables you to control what the user sees when interacting with the Records List Properties and the Records Detail Page . The following sections list the properties you can configure. For information about how to use Records , see Managing Records .

#### Records List Properties

The Records List Properties displays the submitter's requests, including open, sent back, or closed requests. The following video shows how to update Records List Properties 's visual elements to match your workflow.

The following tables list all the configurables for Records List Properties . Review updates on the Canvas as you make changes.
This table describes the configurables for the group General : | | Property | Description
| ID | Assign a unique identifier value to the widget used to refer to it in the script.
| Name | Accept the default or set a custom name for the Records List Properties page.
| Page Title | Set the page title for viewing the request list. Options are:
- Same as form name

- Same as app name

- Custom name (specified in the Display Text property)
| Display Text | This property is visible when you set Page Title to Custom name . Set a custom page title for viewing the request list.

This table describes the configurables for the group Configure Settings : | | Property | Description
| Status Settings | Review and update the available status options as required. See Status Settings for more information.
| 'Draft' Display Text | When you upgrade your app, unsynced requests are set to a draft state. Accept the default or set the text to indicate the request is a draft version.
| 'Queued' Display Text | When you submit a request and the device is offline, it queues the request for submission upon reconnection. You can accept the default setting or customize the text to show that the request is queued.

This table describes the configurables for the group Field Settings : | | Property | Description
| Select Form Fields To Display | Use the Add Fields button to select all, some, or no form fields to show in the request list.

This table describes the configurables for the group Actions : | | Property | Description
| On Click Of '+' Navigate To | Select the form the app will open when the user taps Plus .

This table describes the configurables for the group Filter & Sorting : | | Property | Description
| Filter |
- Title —Accept the default or set a custom title for the Filter pop-up.

- Select the method used to filter Records content. Accept the defaults or set the text of each filter as required. Tip: Select the Settings icon to enable additional system filters or to create custom filters.
| Sorting |
- Reset —Use to reset the Title and Configure Sorting Text properties to their default values.

- Title —Accept the default or set a custom title for the Sorting pop-up.

- Configure Sorting Text —Select the default method used to sort Records content. Accept the defaults or set the text of each sorting method as required. Use the property handles to drag each sorting method into the desired order.

#### Records Detail Properties

The Records Detail Properties interacts with the details of a single request. The following video shows how you can change the visible elements of the Records Detail Properties to match your workflow by updating their properties.

The following tables list all the configurables for Records Detail Properties . Review updates on the Canvas as you make changes.
| | Property | Description
| Fields | Use the Select Form Fields To Display button to choose fields from your forms to include in the request details. Use the property handles to drag each field item into the desired order.
| Delete |
- Reset —Use to reset the Title and Configure Sorting Text properties to their default values.

- The following properties set text values for their respective buttons, headers, text boxes, and labels:
- Dialog Header

- Dialog Confirm Message

- Dialog Confirm Button Label

- Dialog Cancel Button Label

Related information
- Managing Records

- Building A Business App

# SOTI Snap help — Configuring Status Settings
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/configure_status_settings_2
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/configure_status_settings_2

### Configuring Status Settings

#### About this task

SOTI Snap provides several default status values for various workflow stages; however, some may not suit your needs. For example, a status of under review may be better than pending . With this in mind, you can customize default status values to match your process.
When designing your Business app, access the Status Settings windowfrom the following places :
| | From location | Do the following
| Workflow Canvas |
- Select a Set Status activity in your workflow.

- In Properties , select Set Status Settings .
| Approvals |
- From Approvals List Properties , select Approvals Detail Properties .

- Under Content Settings , select Send Back .

- From Set Status After Sending Back , select Set Status Settings .

To update the available status values from the forms page, perform the following steps:

#### Procedure

- Navigate to the Business app's form.

- From the bottom-left of the app canvas, select the Settings icon. The Manage Status window appears.

- Select the icon to change an existing status or add a new one.
Tip: You can delete a status if it is not required or set it as the default status. When submitted for the first time, the default status is assigned to your record.

- Select Save after completing the status edits.

#### Results

The updated status items display as intended when used or referenced in the Workflow Canvas or within the Approvals messages on the mobile device.

Related information
- Adding a Set Status

- Available Activities

# SOTI Snap help — Creating a New App
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/create_a_standard_app
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/create_a_standard_app

### Creating a New App

Begin creating new applications within SOTI Snap .

#### Before you begin

- Log in to the SOTI Snap console as a user with the Apps permission and all sub permissions under Apps enabled.

#### About this task

Use this procedure to begin creating new applications within SOTI Snap 's App Manager. There are three apps that are available for creation in SOTI Snap . Lockdown apps empower organizations to design tailored kiosk-style applications that can be deployed through SOTI MobiControl that limit user access to approved apps and features on a device. Business apps add an extra dimension to SOTI Snap apps by automating tasks, granting actions, reducing manual errors, and producing an actionable trail of activities. Standard apps enable users to create form-based apps with SOTI Snap to digitize paper-based processes, streamline operations, and increase efficiency. The following app types can be created on SOTI Snap :
- Lockdown App

- Business App

- Standard App

#### Procedure

- Select the Apps menu in the SOTI Snap tray on the left hand side. This displays the App Manager screen listing all the apps.

- From the Apps tab, select from one of the following:
- Select Lockdown App > New Lockdown App
Important: You must use a SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap instance and assign the Lockdown Designer role to a user before creating Lockdown apps. For more information on SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap instances and managing Lockdown Designer licenses, see System Requirements and SOTI Snap Lockdown Designer Licensing .

- Select Business App > Start Fresh , to create a new app from scratch or use SOTI Snap's AI creation tool. You may also Import an App or use the SOTI Pulse Marketplace to user pre built templates.

- Select Standard App > New Standard App .

#### Results

You have started creating a SOTI Snap app based on one of the three app types.

#### What to do next

Continue building your new application. See Building a Lockdown App , Building A Business App or Building A Standard App for more information based on which app type was selected.

Related information
- Welcome to SOTI Snap Help

- Building a Lockdown App

- Building A Standard App

- Building A Business App

# SOTI Snap help — Deleting a Lockdown App in SOTI Snap
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/deleting_a_lockdown_app
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/deleting_a_lockdown_app

### Deleting a Lockdown App in SOTI Snap

#### Before you begin

- Sign in as a SOTI Snap user with the Apps permission enabled. See Users and Permissions for more details.

- Have a pre-existing Lockdown app in SOTI Snap , see Building a Lockdown App for more details.

#### About this task

Use this procedure to delete a Lockdown app from the App Manager in SOTI Snap . SOTI Snap validates the deletion against one or more SOTI MobiControl policies to ensure that the app is not currently in use for device management.

#### Procedure

- In SOTI Snap , open Apps .

- Select the Lockdown tab.

- Locate the Lockdown app you want to delete and select its action menu to open the App Actions menu.

- Select Delete .

- Select a SOTI MobiControl instance to validate whether the Lockdown app is in use.
Note: Required only if the app is published. The window lists all instances where the app is deployed, along with the published version. If the app is unpublished, SOTI Snap skips validation and shows the confirmation window.

- Select Validate . Important: If the SOTI Snap Lockdown app is in use with a SOTI MobiControl policy. An error window appears. Confirm in SOTI MobiControl that the Lockdown is not in use in any policy.

- When SOTI Snap shows the confirmation window, confirm the deletion if the Lockdown app is not currently in use with any SOTI MobiControl policies

- Select Delete .

#### Results

The Lockdown app is deleted from SOTI Snap . If the app was previously published, it is no longer available on the selected SOTI MobiControl instance.

Related information
- Building a Lockdown App

# SOTI Snap help — Dynamic User Manager
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/dynamic_user_manager
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/dynamic_user_manager

### Dynamic User Manager

#### About this task

The Dynamic User Manager feature allows you to designate a selected user's manager as the approver within an app workflow. Select the user from a User List widget in the project, and the manager(s) corresponding to their IdP's hierarchical level receive the workflow approval request in their inbox. Restriction: SOTI Snap supports SOTI Identity LOCAL, LDAP, and Microsoft EntraID directories.
Important: For Manager data to be synced from your IDP to SOTI Snap, Map the LDAP's manager property in the Map Additional User Attributes section. This explicit mapping is not required for EntraID and Local DB. Additionally, while configuring LDAP, set the user attribute as Manager DN .
Warning: Maintain the manager attribute mapping on the IDP with the same character case used in LDAP. Also, make sure that this mapping is correct as it is not possible to edit it later.

#### Procedure

- From the left toolbar, select Project > Workflow .

- Select the User Approval activity.

- From the Properties tab, select the Gear icon, then the Dynamic User Manager icon to choose from a list of user managers automatically. The Dynamic User Manager window appears.

- Select the User List(s) from the available User Lists in the app project.

- Select the appropriate managerial hierarchy level(s) for the User List, then select Add . Note: The configured IdP's hierarchy determines the approver based on the chosen managerial level in Assign Manager for the selected User List.

#### Results

The selected user's managerial hierarchy level appears as %Level1UserlistwidgetnameManager% and/ or %Level2UserlistwidgetnameManager% in the list of approvers. Important: If the selected managers are not in the IdP, the user approval process proceeds based on the advanced permissions settings.

Related information
- Submitter Manager

- Adding a User Approval

# SOTI Snap help — Enrolling Devices into SOTI MobiControl and Installing SOTI Snap with Apps
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/enrolling_and_installing_snap
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/enrolling_and_installing_snap

### Enrolling Devices into SOTI MobiControl and Installing SOTI Snap with Apps

Enroll devices into SOTI MobiControl and install the SOTI Snap Agent with associated apps.

#### Before you begin

- You must have one or more SOTI Snap apps that are published to SOTI MobiControl . See Publishing an App for more information.

- Log into the SOTI MobiControl web console with the View App Policies , Manage App Policies , and View Enrollment Policies permissions.

#### About this task

Use this procedure to enroll Android devices into SOTI MobiControl and install the SOTI Snap Agent and apps. This speeds up preparation of devices for managed deployment and ensures that the SOTI Snap Agent and applications are available immediately after enrollment. Administrators can standardize device setup with SOTI Snap and reduce manual configuration effort.

#### Procedure

- Factory reset a device to enroll into SOTI MobiControl as a Work Managed device. Note: For more information on how to start enrolling devices as Work Managed during setup, see Device Side Actions For Enrollment .

- During the Android setup process, when the Google login page appears, enter afw#mobicontrol in the Email field and select Next to trigger the Android Enterprise setup.

- Proceed through the Android Enterprise setup process. Once SOTI MobiControl has launched, enroll the device using an Enrollment ID generated from a Enrollment Policy in SOTI MobiControl . Note: For more information on creating an Enrollment Policy to enroll devices in SOTI MobiControl , see Creating an Android Enterprise Device Policy .

- Optional: To install the SOTI Snap Agent as an APK, create a profile from the SOTI MobiControl Web Console with a Feature Control configuration that has the following options enabled:
- Install from Unknown Sources

- USB Debugging

Note: For more information on how to create a Feature Control profile configuration in SOTI MobiControl . See Feature Control .

- From the SOTI MobiControl main menu, select Policies > Apps

- Select New App Policy .

- Select Android as the platform and choose Android Enterprise .

- In the General tab, beside App Policy Name , add a name for the app policy.

- Select the Apps tab and select Add New App .

- Add the SOTI Snap Agent to the app policy using the Enterprise Apps or Managed Google Play apps app source.
Note: For more information on adding applications from either of these app sources in SOTI MobiControl , see Android Enterprise | Enterprise App Store or Android Enterprise | Managed Google Play .

- Once the SOTI Snap Agent has been added to the app policy, select New App to add a new application.

- Select SOTI Snap Apps to add a SOTI Snap application. Note: SOTI Snap apps are only available inside the SOTI Snap App Catalog when the SOTI Snap Agent is installed.

- Select from one or more published SOTI Snap apps.

- Select Save to save the apps to the app policy.

- Assign the app policy to the device and any additional devices or device groups. Note: For more details on how to assign profiles in SOTI MobiControl , see Assigning a Profile .

- The SOTI Snap Agent installs with devices automatically enrolled into SOTI Snap . The selected SOTI Snap apps begin to install once the installation process has completed.

#### Results

Devices are enrolled into SOTI MobiControl and SOTI Snap with all associated apps.

#### What to do next

Proceed to test the apps on the devices to confirm all functionality works as required. You may also configure a lockdown profile in SOTI MobiControl to deploy and manage the apps in a lockdown environment. See Lockdown | Using the Configuration for more information.

Related information
- Devices: Publishing and Distributing Apps

- Updating SOTI Snap Apps in SOTI MobiControl

# SOTI Snap help — Global Variables
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/environment_variables/app_environment_variables
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/environment_variables/app_environment_variables

### Global Variables

#### About this task

Simplify app modifications by substituting hardcoded values with global variables in locations where you reuse the same values. You can assign a default value to a global variable and then reference the variable in locations throughout your SOTI Snap app, for example, when defining conditions in Visibility or Requirement rules. You can also use global variables in Blockly. When you change the value of the variable, you edit it in one place, and it updates everywhere.
You can configure Global variables per-app. You must create new global variables for each app.

#### Procedure

- In the Canvas , open Data Connection Rules . Move to the Global Variables tab.

- From the Global Variables tab, you can perform the following tasks: | | Task | Procedure
| Adding a Global Variable | To add and define a new global variable:

- Select New Variable to open the Add Global Variables dialog box.

- Enter a name for the variable. Names should be descriptive and unique within the app.

- Enter a short description for the variable that describes its purpose.

- Enter a default value for the variable. The default value is the value that appears at the start of the first session of an app.

- Choose whether to retain the value of the variable across app sessions. When disabled, SOTI Snap disables the value of the variable each time you close the app.

- Optional: Add to add another variable. Variables are not saved as a set. Once you save the variables, SOTI Snap saves each as a separate entry.

- Select Save to save the global variable and close the dialog box.

Result: Global variables appear in the Global Variables tab of the Data Connection Rules view of the Canvas .
| Editing a Global Variable | To edit a global variable:

- Beside the variable that you want to update, select > Edit to open the Edit Global Variable dialog box.

- Make your changes and select Update .

Result: The referenced locations show the changes after publishing the app.
| Deleting a Global Variable | To delete a global variable:

- Beside the variable that you want to delete, select > Delete to open the Delete dialog box.

- Select Yes to confirm the deletion.

Result: The referenced locations show the deletion after publishing the app.

# SOTI Snap help — Example: Creating a Business App
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/example__create_a_business_app
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/example__create_a_business_app

### Example: Creating a Business App

Feature Focus: Building Workflows with SOTI Snap
The following image shows a simple workflow for making a purchase request.
The workflow applies workflow activities through the following steps:

- A user submits a request via their app.

- The request status is then set to Pending Approval.

- The request requires manager approval to move forward. A specific single user is then identified. If the manager disapproves, the following steps occur:
- The request status is then set to Closed Rejected.

- The requestor receives an email indicating the rejection.

- The process ends .

- If the manager approves, and the cost of the request is less than or equal to $500, the following steps occur:
- The request status changes to Approved.

- The requestor receives an email indicating the approval.

- The process ends .

- If the manager approves and the request's cost is greater than $500, it requires group approval to move forward Only two members of the group need to approve.

- If the group disapproves, the following steps occur:
- The request status is then set to Closed Rejected.

- The requestor receives an email indicating the rejection.

- The process ends .

- If the group approves, the following steps occur:
- The request status changes to Approved.

- The requestor receives an email indicating the approval.

- The process ends .

Related information
- Building A Business App

# SOTI Snap help — Generate SOTI Identity Credentials for SOTI MobiControl
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/generate_credentials_from_soti_mobicontrol
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/generate_credentials_from_soti_mobicontrol

### Generate SOTI Identity Credentials for SOTI MobiControl

#### About this task

Use this procedure to generate SOTI Identity credentials for SOTI MobiControl . This creates a secure set of credentials that are entered in the SOTI MobiControl web console to create a synchronized binding.

#### Procedure

- Log in to the SOTI Identity console as an administrator.

- From the main menu, select Applications .
The Applications dashboard appears.

- In the top-right of the Applications dashboard, select the New Applications icon and then select SOTI MobiControl from the drop-down menu. The Add SOTI MobiControl panel displays.

- In the Add SOTI MobiControl panel, enter the SOTI MobiControl Instance Name .

- Optional: You can replace the default logo by uploading or dragging a new logo into the Application Logo field.

- Optional: You can replace the default description by entering a new description into the Description field.

- Disable Visible on SOTI One Portal to hide the instance from the Portal. Note: By default, the instance is visible on the SOTI ONE Portal.

- Select Add and Generate Credentials .

- The SOTI Identity Configuration sub-panel appears. It contains the SOTI Identity URL, Client ID and Client Secret fields. Select the Copy icon to copy the configuration information to the clipboard.

- Select Done .

#### Results

The SOTI Identity credentials for a new SOTI MobiControl application have been generated.

#### What to do next

Proceed with Binding SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl to enter the generated credentials into SOTI MobiControl .

Related information
- SOTI ONE Integration with SOTI Snap

- Integrating SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl for Lockdown

# SOTI Snap help — Integrating SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl for Lockdown
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/integrating_identity_with_mobicontrol_for_lockdown
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/integrating_identity_with_mobicontrol_for_lockdown

### Integrating SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl for Lockdown

Integrate SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl for Lockdown and authentication with SOTI Snap .
Integrate SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Identity . Complete this integration to allow SOTI MobiControl to authenticate users through SOTI Identity and third-party identity providers such as Microsoft Azure and OKTA. This also lets SOTI Snap for Lockdown applications authenticate via SOTI Identity using either user credentials or Near Field Communication (NFC) tags, ensuring access to specific Lockdown applications tailored to each user's requirements.
Refer to the following tasks to integrate SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl :
- Generate SOTI Identity Credentials for SOTI MobiControl

- Binding SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl

- Assigning SOTI MobiControl Users in SOTI Identity

# SOTI Snap help — Integrating SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap for Lockdown
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/integrating_identity_with_snap_for_lockdown
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/integrating_identity_with_snap_for_lockdown

### Integrating SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap for Lockdown

Integrate SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap for Lockdown and app deployment.

#### Before you begin

Complete the integration of SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl (see Integrating SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl for Lockdown ).

#### About this task

Use this procedure to integrate SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap . Linking SOTI Snap with SOTI MobiControl enables administrators to deploy SOTI Snap apps. Integrating SOTI Snap with a SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap instance enables users to deploy SOTI Snap for Lockdown apps directly to devices in SOTI MobiControl through the use of an App Policy or Lockdown profile. By establishing this connection, administrators streamline app distribution, reduce manual configuration, and ensure consistent deployment practices across all managed devices.

#### Procedure

- Log in to the SOTI Identity console as an administrator.

- From the main menu, select Applications . The Applications section opens.

- Select the SOTI MobiControl application, then select SOTI One Integration in the action bar. The SOTI ONE Integration window opens.

- Select the Integrate SOTI MobiControl With SOTI Snap drop-down, then select the SOTI Snap instance.

- Select Save .

- Select the SOTI Snap application, then select SOTI One Integration in the action bar. The SOTI One Integration window opens.

- Select the Integrate SOTI Snap with SOTI MobiControl drop-down, then select your SOTI MobiControl application.

- Log in to the SOTI MobiControl console as an administrator.

- From the main menu, navigate to Global Settings > SOTI Snap .

- Select Bind .

#### Results

You can now create SOTI Snap lockdown apps or publish SOTI Snap apps through a SOTI MobiControl App Policy.

#### What to do next

Proceed with Creating a New App to create a SOTI Snap app or see Devices: Publishing and Distributing Apps to begin utilizing these integrations with SOTI Snap apps.

Related information
- Creating a New App

- Building a Lockdown App

- Creating an App Policy

- Integrating SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl for Lockdown

# SOTI Snap help — Managing Approvals
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/manage_approvals
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/manage_approvals

### Managing Approvals

#### About this task

You can find completed requests and requests that need your approval in the Approvals tab of the SOTI Snap business app. To access and review the request, perform the following steps:

#### Procedure

- Open SOTI Snap on your mobile device.

- In SOTI Snap , open your custom business app and enter your credentials. The business app opens to show two pages: Approvals and Records .

- Go to the Approvals page. Approvals list all pending and approved requests.

- Tap a pending request. The request opens for review.

- At the bottom of the request, select from the following options: | | Option | Description
| Reject | The request does not proceed. If enabled, you can add notes for the submitter. When creating a business app, see Adding a User Approval and Add a Group Approval about this option.
| Approve | The request advances to the next step in the workflow. If enabled, you can add notes related to the approval. For more information about this option when creating a business app, see Adding a User Approval and Add a Group Approval .
| Send back | The request returns to the submitter for changes and is then removed from Approvals . The requestor can resubmit the request. If enabled, you can add notes for the submitter. When creating a business app, see Adding a User Approval and Add a Group Approval about this option.
| Edit | The approver can edit the request details before approving it.
| More | This option is present on lower-resolution devices and enables users to select any of the options above when they cannot fit on the screen.

Related information
- Requesting Management from the App

- Configuring Approvals

### Managing Records

#### About this task

Find your requests and their status in the Records tab of the SOTI Snap business app. To access and review your request status, perform the following steps:

#### Procedure

- In SOTI Snap , open your custom business app and enter your credentials. The business app opens to show two pages: Approvals and Records .

- Go to the Records page. Records list all your Queued (when offline), Pending , and Approved requests.

- Tap a request to review the submitted data.

- Optional: If you requested while the device was offline, the request opens in edit mode. You can continue to update the request and resubmit it to sync with the SOTI Snap server. The record's status is Queued until the device is online. Once synced, the status changes to Pending .

# SOTI Snap help — Managing App Screens and Navigation
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/managing_app_components
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/managing_app_components

### Managing App Screens and Navigation

After creating an app and adding app components, you may perform further customizations as needed.

#### Procedure

- In the Canvas , select the Project tab.

- For the following available tasks, proceed with the following procedures: | | Task | Procedure
| Editing the Splash Screen | The Splash Screen is the first screen the app user sees when they launch an app to indicate that the app is loading. To edit the splash screen:
- Select Splash Screen (See ① of diagram) to open the Splash Screen Properties panel.

- Enable the Splash Screen switch if you want the splash screen to appear to the app user. Note: The splash screen is only displayed to the app user once per session.

- Set the splash screen background to apply a background color or image, and it automatically reflects on the preview.

- Optional: Select Upload Image to include an image in your splash screen.
| Editing the Header | The header typically remains at the top of the app as the end-user access it. You can configure whether the header is permanently hidden or if it hides itself after a period of inactivity. To edit the header:

- Select Header (See ② of diagram) to open the Header Properties panel.

- Disable the Show App Header switch if you want to always hide the header. Warning: Disabling the app header makes all dependent actions unavailable, including sync data, exit app, view notification, minimize app, and the hamburger menu.

- Enable the Hide on Inactivity switch if you want to hide the header after a period of inactivity.
- Select the length and unit of time before the app hides the header due to inactivity.

- Configure the background color of the header using the Color slider.
- Select Reset to revert the header color to the original. The edits are temporary and unsaved.
| Renaming Forms and Screens | To change the name of a form or screen:
- Hover over the form or screen whose name you want to change.

- Select Edit (See ③ of diagram) to change the name.

- Select the check mark to store the changes. The renaming is temporary and unsaved.
| Rearranging Forms and Screens | To rearrange forms or screens:
- Drag the form or screen to your preferred position. The rearranging is temporary and unsaved.
| Duplicating a Page in Your App | You can duplicate existing pages in your app, which retain the widgets and customizations on the new page. To duplicate a page in your app:
- Hover over an existing page in your app and select Duplicate (See ④ of diagram). The duplication are temporary and unsaved.

Result: The duplicated page appears directly after the original page, with a default name of Copy of <original page name> .
| Deleting Screens | To delete a screen from your app:
- Hover over the screen that you want to delete and select Delete (See ⑤ of diagram).

- Select Delete . The screen deletion is temporary and unsaved.

- The edits are temporary and unsaved. From the top-right, select the general app, select Save to confirm the changes you have made. The changes are now permanent.

# SOTI Snap help — Managing Built Apps
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/managing_built_apps
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/managing_built_apps

### Managing Built Apps

After you have created a new standard or business app, you can execute further actions to the app as a whole.

#### Procedure

- Select the Apps tab. This displays the App Manager screen listing all the apps.

- From an app, select . The App Action menu displays.

- For each of the available app actions, do the following procedure: | | App Action | Procedure
| Search for Action | Enter letters to shrink the App Actions menu to search for a specific App Action.
| Edit | The app's Canvas opens. See Creating a New App for details.
| Preview | Before publishing an app, you can preview and test it. This functional preview demonstrates how the user experiences the user interface of your app and also enables you to make further changes.

- Select the device skin you want to use to view your app. You can search for device skins based on the device manufacturer and model.

- Select Preview to display the app in the selected skin. In Preview mode, you can test the app and fill in the app fields to check app functionality.

- From the Preview mode's top-right corner, select X to exit from the Preview mode and return to the App Manager .
| Publish | See Publishing an App for details.
| Version | SOTI Snap tracks the history of changes made to apps and updates the app's version whenever you publish the app.
- The Version History page displays. This page shows both published and draft versions. You can then execute further app actions on different versions of an app.

- Preview

- Duplicate

- Use as Current Draft

- Deactivate

- Data Export Status

- Report

- Templates

- API Details
| Report | Restriction: This app action is only available after you have published the app,
See Using Reports: Tracking App Responses for details.
| Manage Access | See Managing Access to Your Apps for details.
| Manage Enviroments | See Managing Environments for details.
| Share | See Exporting/Sharing an App with Another SOTI Snap Channel for details.
| Duplicate | To create a duplicate of an app, do the following:

- In the App name field, enter a name for the new app. This is the display name that appears on both the App Manager screen and native app.

- Select Duplicate .

The duplicate app is now listed in the App Manager screen.
The duplicate app inherits its state (Active, Inactive, or Draft) from the original app.
| Delete | Note: When you delete an app, its data, associated versions, and data export rules are also removed from your account.
Note: For Lockdown apps, additional verification may be needed to confirm if the Lockdown app is in use on a SOTI MobiControl instance. See Deleting a Lockdown App in SOTI Snap for more details.
- The Delete App? dialog box displays. Select Delete to confirm the deletion.

Result: Once an app is successfully deleted, SOTI Snap removes the app from the App Manager screen.
| Data Sources | Restriction: This app action is only available.

# SOTI Snap help — Creating and Managing Drafts
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/managing_saved_drafts
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/managing_saved_drafts

### Creating and Managing Drafts

#### Creating A Saved Draft

##### Before you begin

Publish an existing app.

##### About this task

To replace your current draft with a published version, you may save the existing draft beforehand for future reference.

##### Procedure

- On the App Manager view, select the icon for Published environments of the appropriate app.

- For the published app, select More > Use as Current Draft .

- Toggle Save copy of current draft and enter a Draft Name .

- Select OK to save the draft.

##### Results

You can find your draft in the Saved Drafts dialog.

#### Managing Saved Drafts

##### About this task

You can take several actions on saved drafts of published apps by doing the following.

##### Procedure

- On the App Manager view, select the icon for Saved Drafts of the appropriate app.

- For the saved draft you wish to manage, select More . You may perform the following actions on your saved draft:
| Use As Current Draft | Revert the current published app to the saved draft
| Preview | Preview the saved draft of the app.
| Rename | Rename the saved draft of the app.
| Duplicate | Create a duplicate copy of the saved draft.
| Delete | Delete the saved draft.

Related information
- Publishing an App

# SOTI Snap help — Publishing Lockdown Apps
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/publishing_lockdown_apps
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/publishing_lockdown_apps

### Publishing Lockdown Apps

Publish Lockdown apps in SOTI Snap for use with SOTI MobiControl Lockdown profiles.

#### Before you begin

- Log into the SOTI Snap web console with the Apps permission and all related sub permissions enabled.

- You must have a draft of a Lockdown app or create a new app in SOTI Snap . For more information on creating a new app, see Creating a New App .

- Integrate SOTI Snap with SOTI MobiControl to publish apps. See Integrating SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap for Lockdown for more information.

#### About this task

Use this procedure to publish Lockdown apps or home screens created in SOTI Snap for use in SOTI MobiControl . SOTI Snap automatically deploys the application directly to SOTI MobiControl for selection as a Lockdown home screen while creating a new or pre-existing Lockdown Profile.

#### Procedure

- In the SOTI Snap main menu, Select Apps to open the App Manager .

- Under the Lockdown tab, select the name of any Lockdown app to open the canvas menu for the associated app. Tip: You may also select (more) to open the App Actions menu and select Publish . Proceed to step 4 when selecting this option.

- In the canvas menu, select Publish in the top right hand corner.

- In the publish menu, configure various options related to the publishing of the application:

- Select a MobiControl Instance to publish directly to SOTI MobiControl . The application becomes available has a Lockdown app while configuring a Lockdown profile in SOTI MobiControl . Note: If no SOTI MobiControl instances are available for selection. See Integrating SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap for Lockdown for more information on how to link SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap .

- Select a Major or Minor Build Number , all new applications start with 1 as major and 0 as minor but can be changed to meet requirements. Each new version of the application is ordered in ascending order.

- Enter any Notes relating to the Lockdown app in the text field.

- Select Next to continue to Generate Screenshots .

- SOTI Snap proceeds to generate a screenshot of each home screen in the Lockdown app project, the screenshots are sent to SOTI MobiControl to use as visual reference while creating or editing a Lockdown profile. Select each home screen to view a preview screenshot of the corresponding home screen.

- Select Publish to publish the application to SOTI MobiControl .

#### Results

The SOTI Snap Lockdown app has been successfully published over to SOTI MobiControl for use with Lockdown profiles. Important: Avoid deleting published lockdown apps if there are assigned with a SOTI MobiControl Lockdown profile.

#### What to do next

Open the SOTI MobiControl web console and proceed with to begin using the published application as a Lockdown app while creating a Lockdown profile.

Related information
- Creating a New App

- Building a Lockdown App

- Integrating SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap for Lockdown

SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/review_manage_approvals

### Managing Approvals

#### About this task

Use the Approvals tab in your web console to review and manage requests from business app users. Note: Access to the Approvals tab requires permission to Manage My Approvals. See Users and Roles for information about assigning permissions.

#### Procedure

- Navigate to Approval Management . The Approval Management window opens.

- Select the Approvals tab to display a list of pending, approved, rejected, and returned requests.

- Optional: If the list is long, or you want to focus on aspects of the requests, use the filter lists to narrow the results.

- To view the details of an individual request, select the approval Key . The Record Details window opens.

- Select from the following options: | | Option | Description
| Reject | The request does not proceed. If enabled, you can add notes for the submitter. See Adding a User Approval and Add a Group Approval about this option.
| Approve | The request advances to the next step in the workflow. If enabled, you can add notes related to the approval. See Adding a User Approval and Add a Group Approval about this option.

# SOTI Snap help — Adding SOTI MobiControl Script Actions to Widgets
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/running_soti_mobicontrol_scripts_with_lockdown_apps
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/running_soti_mobicontrol_scripts_with_lockdown_apps

### Adding SOTI MobiControl Script Actions to Widgets

#### Before you begin

- You must have a Managed SOTI Snap instance connected with SOTI MobiControl . See Integrating SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap for Lockdown for more information.

- You must have a Lockdown app created in SOTI Snap . See Building a Lockdown App for more information.

- Log in as a SOTI Snap user with the Apps permission enabled and a Lockdown Designer license assigned. See Users and Permissions for more information.

#### About this task

Use this procedure to add a Run MobiControl Script action to a widget event within a SOTI Snap Lockdown app. This action runs a device-side SOTI MobiControl script from the SOTI Snap lockdown home screen when selecting on a widget. Such as clearing the cache, collecting diagnostics or changing device settings. Note: Not all widgets support events that trigger actions. See Available Widgets for the list of supported widgets.

#### Procedure

- In the SOTI Snap main menu, open Apps .

- Locate the app to configure and select it to open SOTI Snap Lockdown Designer .

- In the Lockdown app editor, add or select a widget that supports a onClick event. A context window appears based on the widget selected.

- In the upper-right corner of the context menu, select Events .

- Next to Actions , select (Add) to add a new action.

- Select Run MobiControl JavaScript .

- Select MobiControl Script Editor .

- In the Script Type dropdown, select one of the following options:

- MobiControl Legacy Script : Runs a script that uses the legacy SOTI MobiControl scripting syntax. See Script Commands for more information.

- MobiControl JavaScript : Runs a script that uses the SOTI MobiControl JavaScript feature set. See Android JavaScript Scripts for more information. Important: JavaScript scripts are available only on Android devices.

- Enter a descriptive name for the script.

- In the script editor, enter or paste the SOTI MobiControl script contents. Note: Scripts run with the permissions of the SOTI Snap Device Agent on the device. Test the script in SOTI MobiControl before adding it to a Lockdown app.

- Select Done .

#### Results

The SOTI MobiControl script action is added to the widget. When the event triggers on a device, the SOTI Snap Device Agent runs the configured script.

#### What to do next

Publish the Lockdown app and deploy the updated version to devices to make the action available to users. See Devices: Publishing and Distributing Apps for more information.

Related information
- Building a Lockdown App

- Available Widgets

# SOTI Snap help — Submitter Manager
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/submitter_manager
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/submitter_manager

### Submitter Manager

#### About this task

The Submitter Manager feature enables you to assign the submitter's manager as an approver in an app workflow. Once set up, the workflow request from the submitter is routed to the manager at the IdP's preferred hierarchical level(s). The manager then receives the workflow approval request in their inbox. Restriction: SOTI Snap supports SOTI Identity LOCAL, LDAP, and Microsoft EntraID directories.
Important: For Manager data to be synced from your IDP to SOTI Snap, Map the LDAP's manager property in the Map Additional User Attributes section. This explicit mapping is not required for EntraID and Local DB. Additionally, while configuring LDAP, set the user attribute as Manager DN .
Warning: Maintain the manager attribute mapping on the IDP with the same character case used in LDAP. Also, make sure that this mapping is correct as it is not possible to edit it later.

#### Procedure

- From the left toolbar, select Project > Workflow .

- Select the User Approval activity.

- From the Properties tab, select the Gear icon, then the Submitter Manager icon. The Submitter Manager window appears.

- Choose the appropriate managerial hierarchy level(s), then select Add . Note: Based on the chosen managerial level in the Assign Appropriate Manager Level window, the approval is sent to the corresponding approver based on the hierarchy in the configured IdP.

#### Results

The submitter's selected managerial hierarchy level appears as %Level1Submitter'sManager% and/ or %Level2Submitter'sManager% in the list of approvers. Remember: If the selected managers are not in the IdP, the user approval process proceeds based on the Advanced Permissions settings.

Related information
- Adding a User Approval

- Available Activities

# SOTI Snap help — The Asset Manager
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/the_asset_manager
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/the_asset_manager

### The Asset Manager

The SOTI Snap Asset Manager enables efficient management and organization of digital assets used within SOTI Snap apps, such as images, icons, and configuration files. These assets are essential for customizing the look, feel, and behavior of SOTI Snap apps.

#### Key Functionalities

- Drag image and video files directly into the Asset Manager section, or upload files using a file picker.

- Image formats: .gif , .jpg , .jpeg , .png (maximum size: 10 MB)

- Video formats: .mp4 , .mov (maximum size: 50 MB)

- Total size per upload session: maximum 200 MB

- A progress bar displays while uploading and a confirmation displays upon successful upload.

- Uploaded assets are automatically added to the Asset Manager for easy reuse in widgets and other design elements.

- Once uploaded, assets are available in the Asset Manager as App Media for a specific app.

- You can make app-specific assets available to all apps by marking them as Shared Media.

Note: Previously uploaded media in older apps are not available in the Asset Manager.
Note: Assets stored in Asset Manager are internal to SOTI Snap and you cannot link to them from external sources.

#### Accessing the Asset Manager

To access the Asset Manager:
1. From the main menu, select Apps .
2. In the App Manager view, select an app.
3. In the app view, select Asset Manager .
4. In the Asset Manager panel you can upload assets by dragging files to the Drag and Drop files here or Browse File section (red box below). Select Browse File to upload files using file explorer. Note: You can only add assets in the App Media tab, since an asset's initial context is the specific app the asset is uploaded to. You cannot upload assets directly to the Shared Media tab. Validations While Uploading
| | Validation | Error Message
| Image/video format– .gif , .jpg ( .jpeg ) or .png , .mp4 , .mov | Invalid Format. Supported Formats:Images–.gif, .jpg, .jpeg, .png; Videos–.mp4, .mov
| Max size | File size exceeds the limit. Maximum size allowed: Images–10 MB; Videos–50 MB
| In case of multiple uploads (images or videos) | Maximum size allowed: 200 MB
| In case of duplicate upload | Media file already exists in your library
| Multiple reasons for failure |
- Media file already exists in your library

- File format is not supported

- File size exceeds the allowed limit.

5. Uploaded assets are only available in the selected app. You can mark an asset as shared, making it available across all apps in the Shared Media tab. To share an asset, select the ( ) More icon (red box) for the asset in the App Media tab and then select 'Mark as shared media' (blue box below). The asset now also appears in the Shared Media tab and is available for use by all apps.
Note: In the App Media tab, you can download, rename or delete the asset by selecting the ( ) More icon (red box above) for the asset and choosing the corresponding menu item.
6. Assets marked as shared media appear in the Shared Media tab.
Note: If you delete an asset in the App Media tab after marking it as shared media, the asset is removed from the App Media tab. The asset continues to appear in the Shared Media tab, and stays available to all apps.
Note: You cannot delete assets from the Shared Media tab. Once you mark an App Media asset as shared media, the asset appears in the Shared Media tab. You cannot delete an asset in the Shared Media tab.

Related information
- Building A Standard App

- Building A Business App

- Managing Built Apps

# SOTI Snap help — Activity
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/tsk_activity
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/tsk_activity

### Activity

#### Before you begin

The Activity option is available through the Approval Management's Approvals tab. Note: Access to the Approvals tab requires permission to Manage My Approvals. See Users and Roles for information about assigning permissions.

#### About this task

Use Activity to review the activity of any request within the web console.

#### Procedure

- Navigate to Approval Management . The Approval Management window opens.

- Select the Approvals tab to display a list of pending, approved, rejected, and returned requests.

- Optional: If the list is long, or you want to focus on aspects of the requests, use the filter lists to narrow the results.

- Locate the request you wish to track and select More . A list of options appears.

- Select Activity . The Activity information window opens for the request, showing a timeline of workflow activities and outcomes.

#### Results

You have selected and reviewed the activities associated with an approval request.

Related information
- Managing Approvals

- Request Management from the Web Console

- Approval Management

# SOTI Snap help — Example: Setting up and Using a Gridview Layout
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/tsk_example_setting_up_and_using_a_gridview_layout
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/tsk_example_setting_up_and_using_a_gridview_layout

### Example: Setting up and Using a Gridview Layout

#### Before you begin

To try the example below, you must have done the following:
- Selected a Gridview layout

- Created a page displaying a Business Card Reader widget

- Created a page displaying a QR Code widget

- Added buttons with "Business Card" and "QR Code" as Display Text

#### About this task

The following example shows how to set up a Gridview layout to dynamically change its content when the user selects a button or image. The Gridview layout works with a widget's On Click event.

#### Procedure

- From the app page, select the Business Card button. The Button Properties tab opens.

- Select the Events tab.

- From Events , select On Click.

- From Actions , select Add . A list of actions opens.

- From the list, select Display Page in a Window. The configuration options for the action display and numbers for each grid section appear.
The purpose of configuring this action is to replace the content in a section of the Gridview with content from another page when the user selects the Business Card button.

- From Display in Window , select the Gridview section number of the form where the new content goes.

- From Page to Display , select the page to display.

- Select Add .

- Repeat steps 1 to 8 for the QR Code button.

- Select Save in the SOTI Snap banner to apply your changes.

#### Results

You have completed the Gridview layout example and can dynamically change its content through user interaction. Tip: Select Preview to see the user's experience of how the Gridview layout behaves in your app.

Related information
- Gridview Layout

# SOTI Snap help — View and Reassign Approvals
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/tsk_manage_approvals
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/tsk_manage_approvals

### View and Reassign Approvals

#### Before you begin

To reassign workflow approvals, approval administrators must have the following Approval Management permissions:
- View All Approvals

- Reassign Approvals

To modify the rights for a user's role, see Modify General Permissions .

#### About this task

Use SOTI Snap Approval Management to reassign approvers for individual or multiple workflow approval requests. The ability to reassign approvers is valuable if the current approver becomes unavailable, inactive or if there are organizational role changes.

#### Procedure

- Navigate to Approval Management . The Approval Management window opens.

- Select the All tab to display a list of unapproved requests.

- Optional: If the list is long or you want to focus on aspects of the requests, use the filter lists to narrow the results.

- Optional: To assign a single approval request to a user, select More > Assign Approver beside the approval request you want to change. The Assign Selected Approval(s) to New Approver window opens.

- Optional: To assign multiple approval requests to a user:
- Select for each approval request to reassign.

- Select Assign Approver . The Assign Selected Approval(s) to New Approver window opens.

- From the Assign Selected Approval(s) to New Approver window, select Approver and choose an approver from the list.

- Optional: Select Notify new approver by email to alert the new approver to the selected approval requests.

- Select Assign .

#### Results

You have reassigned the selected approval requests to another approver.

Related information
- Approval Management

# SOTI Snap help — Using Navigator: Navigate Widgets and Customizations
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/tsk_navigate_widgets_and_customizations
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/tsk_navigate_widgets_and_customizations

### Using Navigator: Navigate Widgets and Customizations

#### About this task

Searching for a specific element within an app project can be time-consuming, especially in large and complex projects. The Navigator window is a single interface for selecting among widgets, Blockly code, Java scripts, and rules without the need for manual searching. Follow the instructions below to customize the size and position of the Navigator window and to locate the specific elements you need. Note: The Navigator window lists items in the order they appear on each app page.

#### Size and Drag the Window

##### About this task

Place and scale the Navigator window as required on the canvas.

##### Procedure

- To move the Navigator window, select the top of the window and hold your left mouse button down.

- Drag the window to the desired location and release the button.

- To resize the window, select the bottom-right corner, and drag the boundary until you have your preferred size. Note: To reset the Navigator window to its original location and size, select the Navigator button to close and reopen it. The Navigator window also resets each time the canvas opens.

##### Results

The Navigator window's size and location match your needs.

#### Search and Select

##### About this task

The Navigator window streamlines how you locate specific widgets, Blockly code, JavaScript, or rules. The Navigator window lists all the configurable elements of your project. Selecting one takes you to it directly. Searching narrows the results in the window, making navigation easier still.

##### Procedure

- Optional: Find on a page —If you know the form or page where an element is, you may filter elements using the form and page list:
- From the Navigation window, select the form and page list to reveal the project's forms and pages.

- From the list, select a form or page. The items shown in the Navigation window update to reflect all the elements on the selected form or page.

- Select an element (such as a widget, Blockly code, JavaScript, or visibility rule) from the list.

- Optional: Search by name or type —You may also find app elements by searching based on a widget's name or type.
- From the Navigation window, select the Search field.

- Type part or all of the widget's Name property or type (for example, "text box"). The list updates as you type, narrowing the results. Note: The Search field only searches for widgets. If the widget has Blockly code, JavaScript, or a rule, it is visible and selectable under the widget. You cannot search for non-widget elements directly.
Note: The search applies to all widgets in the app. Selecting a form or page does not narrow the search scope.

- Select an element from the search results.

##### Results

The environment navigates to the element or its parent widget and gives it focus. The Properties , Format , and Events tabs update to reflect the element. If the element is Blockly code, JavaScript, or a rule, the editors for those elements open.

# SOTI Snap help — Selecting a Gridview Layout
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/tsk_select_a_gridview_layout
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/tsk_select_a_gridview_layout

### Selecting a Gridview Layout

#### About this task

Gridview layout options are available when creating a new app, form, or page. In each case, the options for dividing the viewable space are the same.
Note: When creating a new app, the Gridview layout applies to the default page.
Note: You can not place a Gridview layout within another Gridview layout.

#### Procedure

- From the dialog window for a new app, form, or page, select from the available options to divide its workspace. Notice: For the available Gridview / Page Layouts options, you can later change all except the No Grid option. If you choose No Grid and decide later you want to change the layout, use the Layout Widget .

- Complete the required fields for the app, form, or page. Select Save and Continue if this is a new app or Add if you are adding a new page or form.

- Open the page or form that has the Gridview layout.

- Enter an ID and Name for the Gridview layout, or keep the defaults.

- From the Container section, adjust its properties as required:
Table 1 . Gridview Container Properties | Presets | Sets the number and arrangement of the panels.
| Show Border On Device | Turn on to make the grid's borders visible during runtime.
| Layout | Move the internal borders of the Gridview layout to suit the contents and requirements of each area.
| Fill | Select a background fill option for the Gridview:
- Color —Select a color from the Color palette to use as the grid's background.

- Image —Select Upload Image to import a graphic as the grid's background. From Scale , select a display option for the image:
- Fit

- Fill

- Stretch

- Tile

- Center

Select Reset to remove all background choices.

- Optional: Add a visibility rule. Use visibility rules to control when the Gridview is visible to the app user. For example, you can create a condition that the Gridview only appears after the app user fills in a field or submits a form.

- Select Visibility Rule to open the Set Visibility Rule dialog box.

- Select whether the visibility rule requires meeting all or any conditions.

- From the Field list, select an app field or variable and an operator. Enter the value required for the Gridview to be visible.

- Select Add Condition to add another condition.

- Select Save .

- Move to the Format tab to customize the Gridview's appearance.

- Select Save in the SOTI Snap banner to apply your changes.

#### Results

The Gridview is configure for use as required. Tip: Select Preview to see how the Gridview appears in your app.

#### What to do next

Add button or image widgets to the Gridview layout and configure their On Click events to update its contents. See Example: Setting up and Using a Gridview Layout to get started.

Related information
- Example: Setting up and Using a Gridview Layout

- Gridview Layout

# SOTI Snap help — Updating SOTI Snap Apps in SOTI MobiControl
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/updating_soti_snap_web_apps_in_soti_mobicontrol
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/updating_soti_snap_web_apps_in_soti_mobicontrol

### Updating SOTI Snap Apps in SOTI MobiControl

Update existing SOTI Snap apps to the latest version in SOTI MobiControl .

#### Before you begin

- You must have an app policy created with SOTI Snap apps deployed to one or more devices or device groups. To create an app policy, see: Enrolling Devices into SOTI MobiControl and Installing SOTI Snap with Apps .

- Log into the SOTI MobiControl web console with View Enterprise Apps and Manage Enterprise Apps permissions.

#### About this task

Use this procedure to update existing SOTI Snap apps deployed in SOTI MobiControl . Updating ensures devices receive the latest version of each app, which may include new functionality, performance improvements, and security fixes. Administrators can install updates from the Enterprise App Store in SOTI MobiControl without reconfiguring app policies or redeploying apps manually.

#### Procedure

- From the SOTI MobiControl main menu, select Policies > Apps .

- In the top-right corner, select Enterprise App Store .

- Select the SOTI Snap Apps tab.

- Locate the SOTI Snap Web App and select Update in the Available Version field (visible when a new version is available).

- On the device or in the SOTI MobiControl web console, perform a device check-in with the SOTI MobiControl Agent.

- Verify that the SOTI Snap app updates to the latest version.

#### Results

The SOTI Snap app updated to the latest available version from SOTI MobiControl . All devices that currently have the application installed begin to install the new version directly from SOTI MobiControl .

#### What to do next

Confirm on the devices that the SOTI Snap app is running the updated version.

Related information
- Devices: Publishing and Distributing Apps

- Enrolling Devices into SOTI MobiControl and Installing SOTI Snap with Apps

# SOTI Snap help — Using Bottom of Canvas Toolbar
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/using_canvas_features
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/using_canvas_features

### Using Bottom of Canvas Toolbar

#### About this task

Normally SOTI MobiControl displays a device preview in the canvas.
For apps designed for an unique circumstance (such as a specific device or a specific resolution), you can use the tool bar at the bottom of the canvas. This tool bar offers the following features:

- Switching between device orientation

- Switching between device type

- Enabling widget Scaling

- Selecting a specific manufacturer and model

- Selecting a custom resolution

- Removing a device skin

#### Procedure

- Select the tab at the bottom of the canvas to reveal the tool bar options.

- For the following available tasks, proceed with the following procedures: | | Task | Procedure
| Switching between device type layout | You can switch between a phone device layout or a tablet device layout.

- From the leftmost panel, select the or icon to switch to a phone layout. Likewise, select the or icon to switch to a tablet layout.
| Switching between device orientation | You can switch between a landscape or portrait device orientation for your app layout.

- From the leftmost panel, select the icon to alternate between device orientation.
| Enabling Widget Scaling | Before you begin: This procedure is for app creators designing for a specific device or custom resolution, and requires a device skin. About this task: Target devices may display your app using different dimensions than in the IDE, producing unexpected results, and requiring rework and republishing. Enable widget scaling to guarantee consistency in your app's resolution from design to implementation.
Note: Scaling applies to the selected device skin only. Results may be undesirable when published to devices with unknown or varying resolutions. For example, fonts and input boxes may appear too large or too small.

- Select Enable scaling .

- Drag the slider to an appropriate scale for the selected device skin. Note: Widget sizes adjust to match the chosen scale.

- Select Save in the SOTI Snap banner to apply your changes.

Results: Tip: Select Preview to see how the widget appears in your app.
| Selecting a specific manufacturer and model | You can choose the skin for the manufacturer and model of your device.

- From the rightmost panel, select the skin icon. The Skin Selector dialog opens.

- From Manufacturer , select a device manufacturer.

- From Model , select the device model whose skin you require.

- Select Apply .

Results: The app canvas shows all widgets within the selected device skin.
| Selecting a custom resolution | If you cannot find a suitable manufacturer and model for the device skin, you may create a custom resolution.

- From the rightmost panel, select the skin icon. The Skin Selector dialog opens.

- From Manufacturer , select a Custom size .

- From Custom Width , give the pixel width of your device.

- From Custom Height , give the pixel height of your device.

- Select Apply .

Results: The app canvas shows all widgets within the device's custom resolution skin.
| Removing a device skin | If a device skin is no longer required, you may remove it and return to the default device resolution.

- From the rightmost panel, select the removal icon.

Results: The app canvas shows all widgets within the default resolution skin.

# SOTI Snap help — Using Workflow Variables
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/using_workflow_variables
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/using_workflow_variables

### Using Workflow Variables

When you create a new workflow, it initializes with a Start and End activity without any Workflow Variables . You can add and use these workflow variables within certain Activities for better customization of your workflow.
Select Add Variable to begin adding new variables. From the Add Workflow Variable dialog, enter the following:

- Variable Name : Enter a name for the workflow variable.

- Description : Enter a description for the workflow variable.

- Value : Enter the value for the workflow variable.

The workflow variables you add are local only to the workflow you created them in. Other workflows for the same app do not have access to the newly created workflow variable. You may also (edit) or (delete) the workflow variable as needed. You can then add the following Activities to interact with workflow variables:

- Rest API : Map workflow variables to REST API Request queries or map REST API Response queries to workflow variables.

- Script : Get or set the value of workflow variables as part of a JavaScript code.

- Set Value : Map workflow variables to your app form fields.

- Condition : Use workflow variables as part of a condition to select the next activity in a workflow.

You can use these four Activities together to leverage workflow variables and customize your overall workflow.

Related information
- Adding a REST API

- Adding a Script

- Adding a Set Value

- Adding a Condition

# SOTI Snap help — View Audit Logs
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/apps/build/view_audit_logs
SOTI Snap · help documentation · apps/build/view_audit_logs

### View Audit Logs

#### Before you begin

You must have Report permissions as part of your access rights to the app.

#### About this task

SOTI Snap maintains a log of all workflow activities for each app. To view the logs, perform the following steps:

#### Procedure

- Open the Apps view. Beside the published app whose audit logs you want to review, select More > Report . SOTI Snap creates a record for each completed workflow.

- In the Reports Viewer tab, select the record whose audit log you wish to view.

- At the bottom of the form, click Audit Logs . The Audit Logs window opens. Note: The audit log option is unavailable if more than one workflow record is selected.

- The Audit Logs window shows a summary of workflow activities.

- Select Advanced to review events for Edit , Approvals , and Emails .

- From Edit , review the list of edit events in the workflow.

- Select to view the details of any edit row.

- Select the Approvals tab to view approvals given as part of the workflow.

- Select Comments to view responses provided by the approver. Note: Approvers may not be required to provide response notes. See content about enabling notes in Add a Group Approval and Adding a User Approval for more about setting response requirements by approvers.

- Select the Emails tab to review emails sent as part of the workflow.

- Select View to read email content.

Related information
- Building A Business App

To review the activity of your business app, perform the following steps:

- In SOTI Snap , open your custom business app and enter your credentials.

- At the top of the form, tap the More button. The following options appear:

- Tap View Logs . A timeline of workflow app events appears.
