Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/connections/update_email_connection
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/connections/update_email_connection

### Updating an Email Server Connection

#### About this task

To update an existing Email Server connection in SOTI Snap Link , do the following:

#### Procedure

- On the Configurations page, select Manage Connections .

- Open the connection you want to edit.

- In the Connection Name field, enter a friendly name that you can use to identify this connection later. Restriction: Connection names must be unique within the SOTI Snap Link console.

- Enter the SMTP details for the email server. | Host Name | Enter the server name or IP address of your SMTP server and its matching port number.
| Username | Enter the username. This can be an email address within the email server.
| Password | Enter the password that corresponds to the username.

- Enter Sender Details . | Name | Enter a name.
| Email address | Enter an email address.

- Enter email addresses for the email recipient.

- Select Test Connection to verify that connection details are correct. SOTI Snap Link sends a test email to the email address entered in the Email Receivers field.

- Select Save to save the connection.

Related information
- Adding/Updating an Email Destination

# SOTI Snap help — Updating Microsoft SQL Server Connection
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/connections/update_sql_connection
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/connections/update_sql_connection

### Updating Microsoft SQL Server Connection

#### About this task

To update an existing SQL Server connection in SOTI Snap Link , do the following:

#### Procedure

- On the Configurations page, select Manage Connections .

- Open the connection you want to edit.

- Fill in the connection details for the SQL Server database. | Connection Name | Enter a name for the connection. It should be unique within this SOTI Snap Link console.
| Server Name | Enter the name of the server where your database resides. Note: Microsoft SQL Server databases without public APIs must be on the same computer or network as SOTI Snap Link .
| Database Name | Enter the name of the database where you want to export the app data.
| Username | Enter the username. SOTI Snap Link supports only SQL server authentication. The account must have read, write, and update permissions for the database.
| Password | Enter the corresponding password.

- Select Test Connection to verify that connection details are correct and SOTI Snap Link can connect to the specified SQL server.

- Select Save to save the connection.

Related information
- Adding/Updating a SQL Server Destination

# SOTI Snap help — Common App Changes that Affect the SOTI Snap Link Console
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/overview/app_changes
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/overview/app_changes

### Common App Changes that Affect the SOTI Snap Link Console

When you publish a new version of an app, any changes that you made are reflected in the SOTI Snap Link console. If you changed the app name or added or removed fields, these changes affect the SOTI Snap Link .
| | Change in SOTI Snap Console | Result in SOTI Snap Link Console
| Change the app name | The app name changes in the SOTI Snap Link . Note: Historical versions of the app will also display the changed name.
| Change the details of the SOTI Snap Link data export connection | The data export rule changes to match its new settings. Note: Significant changes to the data export such as changes to the selected form or app fields may result in failed records so always check the SOTI Snap Link console after you publish an update with major changes.
| Deactivate the SOTI Snap Link data export connection | The data export rule disappears from the SOTI Snap Link console. If you reactivate this rule, it will reappear but loses all of its previously configured destinations.
| Delete the SOTI Snap Link data export connection | The data export rule disappears from the SOTI Snap Link console.
| Deactivate the app | The app remains in the SOTI Snap Link , frozen at its last version.
| Delete the app | The app remains in the SOTI Snap Link , frozen at its last version.

# SOTI Snap help — Apps in SOTI Snap Link
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/overview/apps
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/overview/apps

### Apps in SOTI Snap Link

After an app is published, it appears in the SOTI Snap Link console as specified in its SOTI Snap Link data export connection. Therefore, if you connect multiple apps to the same SOTI Snap Link account, they will all appear in that SOTI Snap Link console.
Each app has at least one rule. Rules are the data export connections that you configured for the app in the SOTI Snap console. Since rules reflect data connections for an app, it's possible for a single app to have multiple rules, either for different forms within the app, the same form with different settings, or both. If you have multiple forms in your app and you want records from all of them, you'll have to create multiple data export rules in SOTI Snap .
Note: App data is delivered according to the settings of the data export rule. If you excluded fields or filtered records, they won't show up in the destinations at all.
Rules must have at least one destination, which controls where app data is sent and the format it's sent in. Destinations are specific to the rule they were created for; though you may duplicate destination settings for different rules, changes to one destination won't affect the other.
Destinations provide granularity to control how app data is rendered in your local data store. Say you have a SQL Server database where you want to save app data. But how you want to present the data differs depending on the form or app field. You can create a destination that exports data from Form A in one format and then create another destination that submits data in another format for Form B.
Note: When you publish an app, changes that affect SOTI Snap Link are reflected in the SOTI Snap Link console. Whenever you publish, especially if you've updated or deleted a data export rule or app field, check the SOTI Snap Link console to make sure the destinations and their settings are still applicable and working. You can see historical versions of app, their rules and destinations, but you cannot edit them.

# SOTI Snap help — Installing and Activating SOTI Snap Link
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/overview/setup_snaplink
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/overview/setup_snaplink

### Installing and Activating SOTI Snap Link

#### Before you begin

- Check the system requirements. See System Requirements

- Create a SOTI Snap Link connection. See Creating a SOTI Snap Link Connection .
- Retrieve the SOTI Snap Link connection's username and password.

#### About this task

To install and activate SOTI Snap Link on your computer, do the following:

#### Procedure

- If you have already downloaded the installer and saved the respective enrollment ID, skip this step.
- From SOTI Snap 's left side navigation pane, select the Settings > SOTI Snap Link tab.

- Beside the connection, select and select Edit .

- Beside Enrollment ID , select to save the enrollment ID for later.

- Select Download .

Restriction: You can't mix and match the enrollment IDs from one SOTI Snap account with a SOTI Snap Link installer from another SOTI Snap account. They must be from the same account.

- Extract the files in the SOTI Snap Link .zip folder.

- In the SOTI Snap Link folder, run SnapLink.Installer.bat as an administrator. If you get a Microsoft Defender SmartScreen warning, select More info , then Run anyway to proceed.

- Follow the instructions of the SOTI Snap Link installation wizard. SOTI Snap Link is now installed on your computer.

Activate SOTI Snap Link

- Open the SOTI Snap Link console. By default, the installer adds a Desktop shortcut for SOTI Snap Link , but if you opted out, you can open it from the installation folder. The default is C:/Program Files (x86)/SOTI/SOTI Snap Link .

- Enter the copied enrollment ID (from earlier) of the SOTI Snap app whose data you want to capture and select Next .

- Read the license agreement and then select I accept the terms in the License Agreement and select Next .

- Enter the Username and Password you retrieved earlier.

- Select Login .

#### Results

SOTI Snap Link is now active. Published Apps with a SOTI Snap Link data export connection using this SOTI Snap Link account appear automatically in the Configurations view.

#### What to do next

Set up a connection. Connections are external locations such as SQL and email servers where you can direct the data exported from your apps. See Connections .

Related information
- Publishing an App

- Connections

# SOTI Snap help — SOTI Snap Link
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/overview/start
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/overview/start

### SOTI Snap Link

SOTI Snap Link is a data export connection that delivers exported app data to internal locations that aren't accessible by public APIs.
A SOTI Snap Link data export starts out like any other data export connection: you create a data export rule in the SOTI Snap console that controls how app data is exported to the specified destination. Where SOTI Snap Link shines is that after you install a local component, the SOTI Snap Link console, you can add multiple destinations for that app data.
Once SOTI Snap Link is set up, app data is routed through SOTI Snap to the destinations configured in SOTI Snap Link .
Note: App data is saved or deleted in the SOTI Snap console according to the publication settings of each app.
Each SOTI Snap Link connection supports only one user login, and will disconnect you after 30 minutes of inactivity.

# SOTI Snap help — Adding/Updating an Email Destination
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/rules/add_email_destination
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/rules/add_email_destination

### Adding/Updating an Email Destination

#### Before you begin

Set up an Email Server connection.

#### About this task

Destinations are where you send the data extracted from an app form. You can send data from one app to many destinations. Data export rules configure destinations. That is, when you created the data export rule in the SOTI Snap console, the destination receives only data as filtered by that rule is available.
To set up or update a destination:

#### Procedure

- On the Configurations page, expand the app and select Configure under the Destinations heading.

- Do one of the following: | | Task | Procedure
| Creating a new email destination |
- Select Add Destination

- Select the Email radio button.
| Updating an existing email destination |
- Open the destination that you want to edit and update the settings you want to change.

- Choose an email Connection from the dropdown list.

- SOTI Snap preselects the Form based on the data export rule and non-editable.

- Enter the email addresses of the recipients in the To field. You can add macros to dynamically generate a list of recipients depending on the contents of the record. For example, assume there's an email address field in the app. You can add a macro that automatically populates the To field with the email address entered into that field.

- Enter a subject line for the email message. You can add macros to dynamically generate more specific subject lines based on the contents of the record.

- Optional: Add any attachments you want to include in the message. Choose a macro (also called 'merge field') from the macro list and you can add data such as captured images as attachments to the email message. If you choose a camera widget in the Attachment field, you have the option of exporting photos in PDF format by selecting the Export in PDF Format checkbox. Many photos from the same camera widget are then combined into a single PDF file. The generated PDF file name has a maximum length of 256 characters with the following syntax: Email Subject_WidgetID .

- Enter the body text of the message. You can add macros to dynamically generate more specific message content based on the contents of the record.

- Select Save to save your settings.

#### Results

SOTI Snap exports data from the app to the specified destination according to the settings you choose both here and in the SOTI Snap data export rule.

Related information
- Adding/Updating a SQL Server Destination

# SOTI Snap help — Adding/Updating a SQL Server Destination
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/rules/add_sql_destination
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/rules/add_sql_destination

### Adding/Updating a SQL Server Destination

#### Before you begin

Set up a SQL Server connection.

#### About this task

Destinations are where you send the data extracted from an app form. You can send data from one app to many destinations. Data export rules configure destinations. That is, when you created the data export rule in the SOTI Snap console, the destination receives only data as filtered by that rule is available.
To set up or update a destination:

#### Procedure

- On the Configurations page, expand the app and select Configure under the Destinations heading.

- Do one of the following: | | Task | Procedure
| Creating a new SQL destination |
- Select Add Destination

- Choose SQL Server as the destination type.

- Choose a SQL Server Connection from the dropdown list.
| Updating an existing SQL destination |
- Open the destination that you want to edit and update the settings you want to change.

- Choose a new SQL Server Connection from the dropdown list.

- Enter a name for the destination. Since you can create many destinations for the same connection, it is important that the destination name is descriptive.

- Enter an INSERT INTO SQL statement into the Insert Script box to specify how to add the data from the app to the database. Important: If you do not add an INSERT INTO SQL statement, the destination does not receive any exported app data.
Tip: You can add macros to the script so the data changes dynamically. Select Macros in the top-right corner of the field to see available macros.

- Enter an UPDATE SQL statement into the Update Script field. This script will update existing records in the destination if their data changes. If you do not add an UPDATE SQL statement, modified records are not sent to the destination. Tip: You can add macros to the script so the data changes dynamically. Select Macros in the top-right corner of the field to see available macros.

- Select Save to save your settings.

#### Results

SOTI Snap exports data from the app to the specified destination according to the settings you choose both here and in the SOTI Snap data export rule.

Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/rules/app_rule_versions

# SOTI Snap help — Disable a Destination
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/rules/disable_destination
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/rules/disable_destination

### Disable a Destination

#### About this task

When you disable a destination, no data is relayed from the app to the destination.
To disable a destination:

#### Procedure

- On the Configurations page, expand the app and click Configure under the Destinations heading.

- Beside the destination you want to disable, set the toggle to the Off position.

- On the Configurations page, expand the app and click Configure under the Destinations heading.

- Beside the destination you want to activate, set the toggle to the On position.

To disable a destination, set the toggle to the Off position.

# SOTI Snap help — Fix Failed Records
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/snaplink/rules/retry_failedrecords
SOTI Snap · help documentation · snaplink/rules/retry_failedrecords

### Fix Failed Records

#### About this task

A failed record is a record that was not properly delivered to the destination configured for that app/form. When a record fails, you can try to manually fix it. A record may fail to be delivered for many reasons such as a network disruption. If this does not work, wait a few minutes and then try again.
If a lot of records begin to fail, check the destination and its connection details.
To attempt to fix a failed record

#### Procedure

- On the Configurations page, expand the app with the failed records and click the red down arrow. Failed records are grouped by data export rule so if you have multiple rules in an app, you'll need to try to fix each rule separately.

- Click Retry all to attempt to fix all records at once or use the individual Retry buttons beside each record to try to fix them individually.

#### Results

If the retry is successful and the record is delivered to its destination, the record will disappear from the list.
If a record continues to fail, SOTI Snap Link will automatically disable the destination.

# SOTI Snap help — SOTI ONE Integration with SOTI Snap
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/SOTIONEIntegration
SOTI Snap · help documentation · SOTIONEIntegration

### SOTI ONE Integration with SOTI Snap

Integrate SOTI Identity , SOTI MobiControl , and SOTI Snap to support authentication, configuration, and app deployment.
SOTI ONE integration connects SOTI Identity , SOTI MobiControl , and SOTI Snap to enable secure authentication, centralized configuration, and streamlined deployment of SOTI Snap applications and policies through SOTI MobiControl by creating a SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap instance. These integrations also streamline workflows for building, configuring, and distributing SOTI Snap for Lockdown experiences across a device fleet.

#### SOTI ONE Integration Process

Integrating SOTI ONE with SOTI Snap requires performing two steps:

- Integrating SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl connects various authentication services to device and console management. This enables SOTI MobiControl to use centralized credentials, assign administrator access, and enforce secure authentication for Lockdown management tasks.

- Integrating SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap enables users to deploy and manage SOTI Snap apps and SOTI Snap for Lockdown apps directly through SOTI MobiControl using synchronized instance bindings.

When both integrations are complete, administrators can create and deploy SOTI Snap applications through SOTI MobiControl and manage user authentication via SOTI Identity .

Refer to the following tasks to begin the SOTI ONE integration process:
- Integrating SOTI Identity with SOTI MobiControl for Lockdown

- Integrating SOTI MobiControl with SOTI Snap for Lockdown

# SOTI Snap help — Apps
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/start/app_manager
SOTI Snap · help documentation · start/app_manager

### Apps

The Apps view manages the custom apps built in SOTI Snap from draft through to publication. You'll perform your app development here.
Apps in the App Manager are split into three categories: Personal, Co-Owned, and Shared.
Personal are apps that you created (or cloned). Co-Owned are apps that another user created and chose to share with you. You (and any other users they've shared the app with) can collaborate with them by making changes to the same app. Shared are apps that another user created and allowed you to see but not make changes to. You can clone public apps and make changes to the copy (which appears in your Personal tab).
Each app listed in the App Manager has the following attributes:
| Name | The name of the app
| Status | The current state of the app; published, draft, or inactive.
| Published | The number of environments the app is currently published in; click the icon to manage the environments.
| Saved Drafts | The number of saved drafts of the app; click the icon to manage drafts.
| Modified On | Last modified date.
| Modified By | Last user to modify the app.
| Actions | Click to view available actions for the app.

# SOTI Snap help — Add Graphs to the Dashboard
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/start/dashboard/add_dashboard
SOTI Snap · help documentation · start/dashboard/add_dashboard

### Add Graphs to the Dashboard

#### About this task

Graphs in the Dashboard view provide quick visual updates on the statistics of your SOTI Snap environment. The Dashboard is user-specific, only you will see these graphs.
To add a graph to your dashboard:

#### Procedure

- Open the Dashboard view.

- Click New Graph .

- Select a graph to add to the Dashboard view. Hover over the graph you want to add and click Add . | Total Apps | Displays the number of apps currently available.
| Platform | Displays (in a donut chart) the proportion of enrolled devices per platform (Android or iOS).
| App Status | Displays (in a donut chart) the number and proportion of apps in Active, Inactive, or Draft states.
| Total Enrolled Users | Displays the number of total users enrolled in this SOTI Snap channel.

- Click Add to save your graphs to your Dashboard view.

- Choose a layout for the Dashboard view.
- Click Configure graph layout .

- In the Graph Layout dialog box, choose a layout: single, double, or triple column.

- Click Apply .

- Drag and drop the graphs around the Dashboard view to arrange them to your liking.

#### Results

Graphs in the Dashboard view update automatically whenever changes are made to your apps or enrolled users.

### Getting Started

Getting started with SOTI Snap is simple, fast, and easy. As a cloud application, setup is minimal, you can access it from anywhere and always receive the latest SOTI Snap features immediately.

- Contact SOTI Sales to purchase a SOTI Snap license or download a trial version from the SOTI ONE Downloads page.

- Open a browser and log on to your SOTI Snap console using the URL and credentials provided by your SOTI account representative

The SOTI Snap console splits into four main views: Dashboard , Apps , Devices , and Settings . Move between views by selecting their tabs in the left panel. The Dashboard view opens by default, providing at-a-glance information about your SOTI Snap environment.
Learn more about each view at Navigating the SOTI Snap Console .
After configuring the SOTI Snap console (add users, set console security, and so on), you are ready to Building A Standard App !
Tip: Open the Avatar menu in the upper-right and select Video Tutorials to learn how to use SOTI Snap .

# SOTI Snap help — Logs
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/start/logs
SOTI Snap · help documentation · start/logs

### Logs

The Logs page in SOTI Snap displays log entries collected from managed devices and from the apps deployed to them. Administrators use the Logs page to review app and device activity, identify errors, and troubleshoot issues without connecting to a device directly.
Administrators request log data from a device running the SOTI Snap Device Agent agent or from a SOTI Snap Link instance. SOTI Snap lists the returned entries on the Logs page.

#### Filters

The filter bar at the top of the page controls which log entries appear in the list. Filters apply to the entries already retrieved.
Table 1 . Logs page filters | | Filter | Description
| Apps | Limits the list to log entries from the selected apps. Select All to include every app or None to clear the selection. Use the Search field to locate a specific app.
| Device | Limits the list to log entries from the selected devices. Select All to include every device or None to clear the selection. Use the Search field to locate a specific device.
| Category | Sets the severity levels included in the list. Select Basic to show Information and Warning entries. Select Advanced to show Fatal and Error entries. Select All to show every entry.

#### Quick Filter

Selecting (Quick Filter) shows a summary of the retrieved log entries grouped by severity, with a count for each severity level. Select a severity card to limit the list to entries at that severity level.
| | Severity | Description
| Fatal | An error that stopped the app or the SOTI Snap Device Agent from operating as expected.
| Error | A failure that affected an operation but allowed the SOTI Snap app or the SOTI Snap Device Agent to continue running.
| Warning | An unexpected condition that did not cause a failure in the SOTI Snap app.
| Information | A record of informational activity from the SOTI Snap app.

#### Obtaining Logs

Selecting Get Logs opens the Get Logs window, where administrators specify the source of the log data to retrieve from a managed device or from a SOTI Snap Link instance. For steps, see Obtaining Logs in SOTI Snap .

Related information
- Welcome to SOTI Snap Help

- Navigating the SOTI Snap Console

# SOTI Snap help — Obtaining Logs in SOTI Snap
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/start/obtaining_new_logs_in_soti_snap
SOTI Snap · help documentation · start/obtaining_new_logs_in_soti_snap

### Obtaining Logs in SOTI Snap

#### Before you begin

- Sign in as a SOTI Snap user with the Logs permission enabled.

#### About this task

Use this procedure to obtain logs from a managed device or from a SOTI Snap Link instance from the Logs page in SOTI Snap . Log requests capture entries from a specific SOTI Snap app and can also include entries generated by the device agent. Use these logs to investigate app behavior, troubleshoot device issues, and confirm that a published app is running as expected in the SOTI Snap environment.

#### Procedure

- In the SOTI Snap main menu, open Logs .

- Select Get Logs .

- In the Get Logs window, choose the source of the log data.

- Select Device to obtain logs from a device running a SOTI Snap app or device agent.

- Optional: If you select Device , use the dropdown to search for and select a device.

- In the Apps section, select (Add).

- Next to Name , use the dropdown to select the SOTI Snap app.

- Use the Environment dropdown to select the SOTI MobiControl environment where the SOTI Snap app is published. For more information, see Devices: Publishing and Distributing Apps .

- Optional: Enable Include Device Level Logs . The request includes log entries generated by the device agent in addition to app log entries.

- Select Get .

#### Results

SOTI Snap sends the log request to the selected source. Select (Refresh) to update the list. The retrieved entries appear on the Logs window.

#### What to do next

Review the logs and use the filters on the Logs window to narrow the results by app, device, category, keyword, or time period. For details, see Logs .

# SOTI Snap help — Set Your Time Zone

### Set Your Time Zone

#### About this task

Choose your preferred time zone within SOTI Snap web console preferences. Use this setting to prevent potential misunderstandings and errors from time discrepancies between the app developers' local workstations and the SOTI Snap web console. Note: SOTI Snap defaults to your device's time zone.

#### Procedure

- From the web console, select the Avatar menu.

- Select Preferences . The Preferences window opens.

- Select Time Zone .

- Scroll through the list and select your preferred time zone.

- Select Save .

#### Results

Activities in the web console now reflect your preferred time zone.

### Configuring Channel Settings

#### About this task

A channel is the organization in charge of a single SOTI Snap instance.
Restriction: Only SOTI Snap administrators can edit channel settings.
To edit channel settings:

#### Procedure

- On the Settings view, select the Channel tab.

- Hover over the image under Channel Logo and select Edit to open a file explorer window and upload a new image to use as the logo for your channel.

- Enter a name for the channel.

- Select Save in the bottom right corner to accept the new channel settings.

# SOTI Snap help — Check Your SOTI Snap Subscription
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/check_subscription
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/check_subscription

### Check Your SOTI Snap Subscription

#### About this task

To quickly review the status of your SOTI Snap subscription:

#### Procedure

- In the upper right corner of the SOTI Snap console, click the dropdown beside your name and select Subscription Info from the dropdown list.

- Review information about your SOTI Snap subscription in the Subscription Information dialog box. | Product | Indicates the current subscription type, Paid or Trial.
| Tenant ID | Displays the identification number for the SOTI Snap tenant.
| Apps Published | Lists how many apps have been published from this SOTI Snap tenant.
| Device License | Shows the number of device licenses you've consumed and your total number of device licenses. To purchase more device licenses, contact your SOTI Snap account manager. You can purchase device licenses add-ons in sets of five device licenses.
| Data Storage | Shows the total amount of storage and how much is currently being used. You automatically receive 1 GB of data per device license. However, you can choose to purchase more data independently of device license number. Contact your SOTI Snap account manager to purchase more data storage space.
| Start date | Displays the day your SOTI Snap subscription started.
| Expiration date | Displays the day your SOTI Snap subscription will expire.
| Server build version | Indicates the SOTI Snap version.
| End-User License Agreement | Click to download the SOTI Snap End-User License Agreement or click End-User License Agreement to open it in a new browser tab.

- Close the dialog box to return to the SOTI Snap console.

# SOTI Snap help — Data Storage
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/data_storage
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/data_storage

### Data Storage

As part of your SOTI Snap subscription, you are allotted data storage space to store app data and records. You get 1 GB of data storage for each SOTI Snap device license.
1 device license = 1 GB of data storage | 100, 000 device licenses = 100 TB of data storage space. Reducing device licenses reduces data storage at the same rate.
You can also purchase additional data storage separately from device licenses, in chunks of 1 GB. Contact your SOTI Snap account manager for details. Updates are reflected immediately in the SOTI Snap console. You can check your data storage limit in the console. SOTI Snap will notify you as you approach your data storage limit, starting with warnings at 80% full.
Data storage is typically consumed by data stored on the app (such as images) rather than by the design of the app itself. To manage your data storage space, you can configure SOTI Snap to automatically delete app records after exporting their data. You can also delete data manually to free up data storage.

# SOTI Snap help — Device Licenses
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/device_licenses
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/device_licenses

### Device Licenses

Device licenses determine how many devices you can enroll in SOTI Snap . Enrolling devices allows you to push the SOTI Snap agent to your users so they can start using your custom-built SOTI Snap apps.
You can add (or remove) device licenses at any time. Contact your SOTI Snap account manager to get started. Additionally, licenses are reusable; each time a device is unenrolled, its license becomes available to use for another device.

# SOTI Snap help — Configuring Device Settings
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/device_settings
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/device_settings

### Configuring Device Settings

#### About this task

To configure how devices and app users interact with SOTI Snap and the SOTI Snap Device Agent:

#### Procedure

- In the Settings view, move to the Device tab.

- For the following available feature, follow the corresponding procedure: | | Feature | Procedure
| Data Sync | Choose how the SOTI Snap Device Agent should synchronize data with the SOTI Snap server.
- Sync data automatically over cellular network: When enabled, SOTI Snap Device Agent data can synchronize while the device has a connection to a cellular network. Otherwise, the SOTI Snap Device Agent only synchronizes data when connected to a WiFi network.

- Sync data while roaming: When enabled, the SOTI Snap Device Agent can exchange data with the SOTI Snap server while the device has a connection to a roaming network. You must enable the Sync data automatically over cellular network to use this option.
| Contact Us | Enter a Phone Number and Email address that app users can use to contact a SOTI Snap administrator when they experience issues. Note: Do not enter contact information for SOTI .
| Security | Choose to enforce HTTPS, apply optional/mandatory PIN or define password complexity for enrollment through email at the device level.
- Enforce HTTPS: When enabled, a device can only use HTTPS-based API calls added in Run Blockly and Run JS actions. SOTI Snap blocks HTTP-based calls (including links to video URLs) and logged in the Device Agent event logs as HTTP call blocked . Note: This setting is applicable to Android devices only. To enforce communication over HTTPS for iOS devices, suggest to app developers to only use HTTPS-based APIs in the app. This setting is only supported in SOTI Snap Device Agent version 3.3 and later.

- Enable PIN: When enabled, app users can choose whether to create a PIN for their SOTI Snap Device Agent.
- Mandatory PIN: When enabled, app users must create a PIN to log into the SOTI Snap Device Agent. Choose how long the PIN must be with the PIN must have dropdown selection.

Note: You can override this setting for individual Enrollment Rules with Enrollment IDs.

- Set Password Complexity: Define password complexity requirements that app users must adhere to if they enroll through email. Note: These settings only apply to email-based enrollment.
| Upgrade | Configure SOTI Snap Device Agent upgrade settings. These settings are global for all devices in this tenant, but you can override them under individual group settings.
- Disable "Optional Upgrade Notification" for SOTI Snap Device Agent: When enabled, the optional upgrade notification does not appear to SOTI Snap Device Agent users. This upgrade action is also disabled on the SOTI Snap Device Agent Settings page.

- Disable upgrade option in "Forced Upgrade Notification" for SOTI Snap Device Agent: When enabled, the forced upgrade notification does not appear to SOTI Snap Device Agent users. This upgrade action is also disabled on the SOTI Snap Device Agent Settings page.
| IDP User Sync |

- Select Save .

#### Results

SOTI Snap updates your device settings. For Data Sync , changes apply to devices the next time when users refresh app catalogs or relaunches the app.

# SOTI Snap help — Configuring General Settings
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/general_settings
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/general_settings

### Configuring General Settings

#### About this task

The General tab on the Settings page allows users to configure custom console settings, including system fonts across the console and SOTI Snap applications.

#### Procedure

- Select Settings from the web console to navigate to the console settings.

- Select the General tab.

- For the following available actions, follow the corresponding procedure: | | Task | Procedure
| Uploading Custom Fonts | Upload a custom font to personalize your console or application. Supported font formats are the following:
- TTF

- OTF

- EOT

- WOFF

- WOFF2

Note: Roboto is the default system font for SOTI Snap . If you delete a custom font, both IDE and the SOTI Snap Device Agent uses Roboto as the font.

Important: SOTI Snap does not assume responsibility for copyrighted fonts uploaded by users. Organizations must verify ownership rights before uploading fonts, as unauthorized use of fonts may have legal consequences.

- Select Fonts to configure font settings.

- Select Add in the Custom Fonts dialog. The Add Custom Fonts window appears.

- Enter a name in the Font Family Name field then select Upload . A file browser window appears.

- Navigate to the font and select it, then select Add .

Results: This page lists the uploaded font family. The user can view an expanded version of the font family by selecting the > icon.

Related information

# SOTI Snap help — Opt-In / Opt-Out Upgrades
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/opt_in_opt_out_upgrades
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/opt_in_opt_out_upgrades

### Opt-In / Opt-Out Upgrades

SOTI typically upgrades all SOTI Snap tenant instances at SOTI 's schedule. When these upgrades happen, expect your SOTI Snap tenant(s) to have anywhere from one to three hours of downtime. The Opt-In / Opt-Out feature enables you to refuse the upgrade or schedule the SOTI Snap upgrades (and the respective downtime) at a preferred date and time of your choosing.
When you log into SOTI Snap and SOTI has an available upgrade for your SOTI Snap tenant, the Software Upgrade dialog box displays.
From this box, you can perform the following operations:

- Next to a new upgrade highlight, select to see more details about the new feature.

- Select a preferred upgrade timeslot from the Select an Upgrade Window dropdown menu, then select Upgrade . This sends an upgrade request to SOTI for the selected upgraded timeslot.

- Select the Remind Me Later button to close the dialog box. When you log in again, the Software Upgrade dialog box automatically appears.

- Select the Don't show this again checkbox, then select Cancel . The Software Upgrade dialog box no longer automatically appears when logging-in again. This is the same as opting out of all software upgrades.

While you have an upgrade available to your SOTI Snap tenant, you have access to the Software Upgrade dialog box. To access the Software Upgrade dialog box without logging out and then logging in, you can perform one of the two following steps:
- In the upper section of the SOTI Snap console, select Upgrade .

- In the upper right corner of the SOTI Snap console, select the dropdown beside your name and select Subscription Info from the dropdown list. Then select Upgrade .

Related information
- Check Your SOTI Snap Subscription

# SOTI Snap help — System Requirements
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/system_requirements
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/system_requirements

### System Requirements

SOTI Snap is a cross-platform mobile app development solution that enables organizations to digitize manual processes quickly and efficiently. It is part of the SOTI ONE Platform , which integrates with SOTI MobiControl for device management and SOTI XSight for analytics. It allows businesses to create custom mobile apps without extensive coding, helping replace paper-based workflows with automated digital solutions. Visit the official SOTI Snap page to learn more about SOTI Snap .
Key Features:
- Drag-and-Drop App Builder: Easily design apps using an intuitive interface.

- Cross-Platform Compatibility: Supports Android and iOS devices.

- Data Collection & Automation: Capture data via camera, barcode scanning, and forms.

- Workflow Integration: Automate approvals and streamline business processes.

- Cloud-Based Deployment: Apps can be instantly pushed to devices.

There are two versions of SOTI Snap :

- SOTI Legacy Snap : The original cloud-based SOTI Snap , for the creation of SOTI Snap apps. When using SOTI Legacy Snap , device management and app deployment remain in the SOTI Legacy Snap console.

- SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap : For the creation of SOTI Snap apps and SOTI Snap lockdown apps. When using SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap , SOTI MobiControl manages devices and deploys apps through the console.

SOTI Snap lockdown apps replace the standard device home screen with a customizable launcher, ensuring users can access only approved applications and settings. This enables SOTI MobiControl administrators to restrict device functionality to a specific set of apps and features. See Lockdown | Using the Configuration for details. Important: Contact your SOTI account manager to request a SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap instance. You can have a maximum of one SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap instance and one SOTI Legacy Snap instance in your environment.

Note: If you have an existing SOTI MobiControl instance. You must link SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap to a SOTI Cloud MobiControl instance. Newly generated instances shall automate a binding between SOTI MobiControl and SOTI MobiControl Managed Snap . For more information on setting up a binding for an existing instance, see SOTI ONE Integration with SOTI Snap .
Note: If you are using SOTI Snap Link to export data (see SOTI Snap Link for details), ensure it meets the SOTI Snap Link System Requirements . Unlike SOTI Snap , which is cloud-based, SOTI Snap Link is an on-premises application and requires installation.

#### Supported SOTI Snap Device Agent Versions

Users can access apps built in SOTI Snap through the SOTI Snap Device Agent , which is available for Android and iOS devices.
| | Operating System | Minimum SOTI Snap Device Agent Version/Download
| Android | 3.0.0 Download from the Google Play Store
| iOS | 3.0.1 Download from the Apple App Store

#### Supported Internet Browsers

Your browser must meet the minimum version requirements to access the SOTI Snap console. For best performance, use the latest version to access the console.
| | Internet Browser | Minimum Version | Recommended Level
| Apple Safari | 10 | Latest version
| Google Chrome | 49 | Latest version
| Mozilla Firefox | 52 | Latest version

#### Supported Device Operating System Versions
| | Operating System | Minimum Supported Version | Maximum Supported Version
| Android | 7.0 | 14
| iOS | 13 | 17

#### Supported Transport Layer Security (TLS) Versions

The minimum supported TLS version is 1.2.

#### IP Addresses

Open the following IP addresses for data connections:

- 18.233.130.79 (Virginia, USA)

- 18.184.65.105 (Frankfurt, Germany)

#### Network Configuration

Open the following ports for the displayed SOTI Snap features and other SOTI products as detailed in the diagram:
| | Component Name | Protocol(s) Supported | TCP Port(s) | Direction
| SOTI MobiControl | HTTP and HTTPS | 443 | Inbound
| SOTI Snap Link | HTTP and HTTPS | 443 | Outbound
| SOTI Snap Link | TCP | 5495 | Outbound
| SOTI Snap Data Access Controller | (HTTP or HTTPS)*** | 5000—5998*** | Inbound
| SOTI Cloud Link Agent | HTTP and HTTPS | 443 | Outbound

Important: SOTI Snap Data Access Controller supported protocols and TCP ports depends on the REST API endpoints configuration created within the SOTI Snap Data Access Controller .

Related information
- SOTI Snap Link System Requirements

- Enroll in SOTI Snap

# SOTI Snap help — Add Groups to Roles
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/user_management/add_group_role
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/user_management/add_group_role

### Add Groups to Roles

#### About this task

Users can be assigned to a role as a group in order to inherit its permissions.

#### Procedure

- On the Settings view, click Groups .

- Select the group(s) you wish to add to roles.

- Click Assign Role .

- Select which roles you wish assigned to the current group(s). Note: Groups can be assigned multiple roles. You can also create a custom role from the Assign Role dialog, though you will have to assign permissions afterwards.

- Click Save .

#### Results

The group has been assigned to the selected roles and has their permissions.

# SOTI Snap help — Add User to Role
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/user_management/add_user_role
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/user_management/add_user_role

### Add User to Role

#### About this task

Users must be assigned to a role in order to inherit its permissions.

#### Procedure

- On the Settings view, click Roles .

- Select the user(s) you wish to add to roles.

- Click Assign Role .

- Select which roles you wish assigned to the current user(s). Note: Users can be assigned multiple roles. You can also create a custom role from the Assign Role dialog, though you will have to assign permissions afterwards.

- Click Save .

#### Results

The user has been assigned to the selected roles and has their permissions.

Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/user_management/modify_general_permissions

- Select the role you want to modify the user permissions for.

- Click General Permissions .

- Select the permissions you want enabled for the users in this role.

The permissions selected are applied to the role.

# SOTI Snap help — Users and Permissions
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/user_management/user_management
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/user_management/user_management

### Users and Permissions

From SOTI Identity (an authentication and authorization service for user management across all SOTI ONE applications.), you can add and manage SOTI Snap console users and groups.
SOTI Identity validates user credentials to verify the authenticity of a user to grant secure access to SOTI ONE products like SOTI Snap . Once users log into a single SOTI ONE application, they are also logged into all applications associated with that SOTI Identity account.
Learn more at SOTI Identity Help .
In SOTI Identity , you can create new Users and Groups, then assign them the SOTI Snap application. Then, through SOTI Snap , you can manage these assigned user and groups.
A default set of roles are provided and additional custom roles can be added, modified, or deleted.

# SOTI Snap help — Users and Roles
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/user_management/users_and_roles
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/user_management/users_and_roles

### Users and Roles

Users in SOTI Snap must be assigned a role to inherit permissions. The following roles are provided by default:

- Administrator

- App Developer

- Development Manager

- Device Manager

- User Manager

Default roles have fixed permissions that can be viewed, but not modified, from General Permissions . To create roles different permission sets, you must create custom roles and modify those accordingly.
Users that have not been given permission to use SOTI Snap in SOTI Identity do not appear in the list of valid users, and cannot be granted access or permissions. When permission to use SOTI Snap is removed from a user in SOTI Identity , that user is also removed from the user list in SOTI Snap .
Users added through SOTI Identity are automatically assigned the User Management (Identity) role.
While user roles can be assigned from both SOTI Identity and SOTI Snap , assignments from SOTI Identity will supersede those from SOTI Snap . Changes to role assignment made in SOTI Snap are not reflected to the assigned roles visible in SOTI Identity .

# SOTI Snap help — Add User Accounts
Source: https://pulse.soti.net/support/soti-Snap/help/?V=2026.1&T=/system/user_management/users
SOTI Snap · help documentation · system/user_management/users

### Add User Accounts

#### About this task

Users and groups for SOTI Snap are created and managed in SOTI Identity , an Identity Provider (IDP) that controls user management across SOTI ONE applications. Learn more at SOTI Identity Help .
Note: This is a high level overview of the steps necessary to add users to SOTI Snap .

#### Procedure

- In SOTI Identity , add user accounts . SOTI Identity supports the creation of local accounts, or you can add LDAP or IDP users from an external identity management system.

- In SOTI Snap , create custom user roles . User roles control access and permissions for SOTI Snap . Each user role maps to a set of permissions, which you will define in the next step.

- Define permissions for the user roles you created in SOTI Snap . Permissions control which sections of SOTI Snap a user role (and therefore all users assigned that user role) can access.

#### Results

Note: You edit user information, deactivate and delete users from within SOTI Identity .
